\end{landscape}
\chaptermark{Chapter 3: Taloon's Road to Riches}

Do not hand order stock to Neta by mistake. Equipment given to the
castle guard counts toward the contract; equipment given to Neta becomes
shop merchandise and must be replaced.

\subsection{Reliable Profit Loop}\label{reliable-profit-loop}

Neta sells merchandise above normal retail value. Use the following loop
regardless of whether Lakanaba stocked a Sword of Malice:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Give Neta one or more valuable weapons or armor pieces from behind the
  counter.
\item
  Leave Endor and remain outside until night.
\item
  Return to the shop and sleep upstairs.
\item
  Speak with Neta from behind the counter the next morning. First
  complete the merchandise prompt, then collect the previous day's
  proceeds.
\item
  Reinvest the profit into additional order pieces or high-value
  merchandise and repeat.
\end{enumerate}

If the Sword of Malice entered Lakanaba's stock, buy as many 3,500G
copies as Taloon can carry. Neta commonly sells each for roughly 5,000G
or more, making this the fastest loop. If it never appeared, use Broad
Swords, Half Plate Armors, fixed cave loot, and monster drops instead.
The guaranteed route is slower but does not depend on a random customer.

The statuette cave remains a good source of both battles and equipment
drops. Preserve the unopened Broad Sword and Half Plate Armor for
Chapter 5 if possible; the royal order can be filled entirely with
purchased copies, and unopened treasure remains available to the
reunited party.

Deliver the order in batches as inventory fills. When the seventh sword
and seventh armor are accepted, the castle pays Taloon \textbf{60,000G}.
Do not spend this payment: it is exactly the amount required for the
tunnel.

\section{Endor-Branca Tunnel and
Casino}\label{endor-branca-tunnel-and-casino}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 12-13\\
\textbf{Required investment:} 60,000G\\
\textbf{Chapter exit:} Cross the completed tunnel and climb the stairs
on the Branca side

Leave Endor by the eastern bridge and enter the first cave on the route
toward the statuette dungeon. Follow its unfinished passage to the old
builder and give him all \textbf{60,000G}. Speak with him once more,
leave the tunnel, and allow approximately one in-game day to pass.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Tunnel Rat (deferred)}

Taloon's investment creates the tunnel required by this achievement, but
it does not unlock during Chapter 3. The Hero will return in Chapter 5
and use the completed passage when its traveler count reaches the
achievement condition.

\end{achievementsetup}

Re-enter the tunnel after a day. Workers should now be present. Speak
with the builder and the workers; one reports that Endor's casino has
reopened. Return to Endor and check with Neta as the construction
advances. When she says the tunnel is complete, the chapter exit is
ready.

The casino beneath Endor's inn is optional. During Chapter 3, one coin
costs \textbf{200G}, an extremely poor exchange rate. Taloon can use the
shared coin balance established in Chapter 2, but the achievement route
still postpones the serious grind until Chapter 5.

Mirror of Ra & 2,500 & Optional; not required for the chapter route. \\
Meteorite Armband & 4,000 & Strong long-term prize, but easier to pursue

Metal Babble Shield & 50,000 & Excellent caster defense and far beyond

\end{guidetable}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: High Roller (deferred)}

The achievement requires a balance of at least 10,000 Casino coins. Do
not buy coins at 200G each solely for this achievement. Preserve any
existing balance and complete the routed poker grind in Chapter 5.

\end{achievementsetup}

\section{Final Inventory Conversion}\label{final-inventory-conversion}

Once the tunnel is complete, Taloon no longer needs combat equipment in
this chapter. Ordinary gold will not transfer to the united party, but
carried equipment will. Convert remaining profits before crossing the
tunnel.

The maximum-value route uses the seven inventory slots not occupied by
the Iron Safe:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  If available, buy \textbf{seven Swords of Malice} for 24,500G total.
  Their normal resale value is 2,625G each, giving the Chapter 5 party
  18,375G of portable value.
\item
  Otherwise, buy \textbf{seven Broad Swords} for 14,000G total. Their
  normal resale value is 1,500G each, giving the Chapter 5 party
  10,500G.
\item
  If Taloon will be used immediately when recruited, keep his best
  equipment instead of maximizing cargo. The recommended equipped set is
  Abacus of Virtue / Iron Apron / Iron Shield / Wooden Hat.
\end{itemize}

Only strip Taloon's equipment after all Chapter 3 combat is complete.
Any gold left after filling his inventory can be converted into casino
coins, but the 200G rate makes this a last resort rather than the
primary carryover plan.

Return home and speak with Neta before leaving. Once she confirms that
the passage is open, say goodbye, rest for free, and save. Enter the
tunnel east of Endor, follow its now-complete linear route, and climb
the stairs at the Branca end.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: The Traveling Merchant}

\textbf{Trigger:} Exit the completed Endor-Branca tunnel on the far side
and complete Chapter 3.

\end{achievementalert}

\section{Chapter 3 Completion Check}\label{chapter-3-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} It's a Trap, The Architect, Moving In, and The
  Traveling Merchant unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Taloon:} Approximately level 12-13, with all required combat
  finished before inventory conversion.
\item
  \textbf{Business:} Endor shop purchased and royal order completed.
\item
  \textbf{Infrastructure:} Endor-Branca tunnel funded, completed, and
  crossed.
\item
  \textbf{Chapter 5 setup:} Tunnel Rat prerequisite established; any
  unopened Broad Sword and Half Plate Armor remain in the statuette
  cave.
\item
  \textbf{Portable value:} Remaining inventory converted to Swords of
  Malice, Broad Swords, or Taloon's preferred equipment.
\item
  \textbf{Carried forward:} Taloon's level and personal inventory, plus
  the shared casino-coin balance.
\item
  \textbf{Not carried forward:} Loose Chapter 3 gold.
\item
  \textbf{Next chapter:} Nara and Mara begin their search for their
  father's killer in Monbaraba.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Chapter 4: The Sisters of
Monbaraba}\label{chapter-4-the-sisters-of-monbaraba}

\section{Section Achievements: Chapter
4}\label{section-achievements-chapter-4}

Chapter 4 follows Nara and Mara, sisters from Kievs who have spent years
seeking Balzack, the former pupil who murdered their father Edgar and
stole his research into the Secret of Evolution. Nara is the steadier
healer and support caster; Mara supplies fire magic and Sap. Both begin
fragile and MP-dependent, so their route rewards defensive equipment,
deliberate spell use, and the early recruitment of a physical companion.

The chapter's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Finish Mara's last performance in Monbaraba, collect the town's hidden
  items, and equip both sisters.
\item
  Visit their home village of Kievs and follow the lead to Edgar's
  surviving pupil.
\item
  Explore the Cave West of Kievs, recover the Lamp of Darkness and
  Sphere of Silence, and recruit Orin.
\item
  Return to Monbaraba with Orin for the earliest \texttt{Private\ Dance}
  opportunity.
\item
  Investigate Keeleon Castle and Haville to learn how the Counselor's
  fear reveals the hidden throne room.
\item
  Enter Aktemto Mine and recover the Gunpowder Jar.
\item
  Frighten the Counselor, follow him to the concealed switch, and defeat
  Balzack.
\item
  Accept the forced defeat against Keeleon, escape prison with the
  Boarding Pass, and sail from Haville.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Chapter 4 Party Development
Plan}\label{chapter-4-party-development-plan}

Nara and Mara retain their levels and personal equipment when they
return in Chapter 5. Orin is temporary and cannot be equipped or
directly controlled. Loose Chapter 4 gold does not carry forward, so
Haville becomes the final conversion point before boarding the ship.

\section{Monbaraba: Final
Performance}\label{monbaraba-final-performance}

\textbf{Starting level:} 1\\
\textbf{Inn:} 3G per person\\
\textbf{Starting payment:} 100G\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} Medical Herb, Strength Seed

Watch Mara's performance in the theater. The owner pays the sisters
\textbf{100G} afterward. Once Nara is under player control, search the
top pot in their house for a hidden \textbf{Medical Herb}.

Explore Monbaraba and speak with its residents during both day and
night. The nightlife provides additional information about Edgar and
Balzack. In the theater basement, enter the rear room and search the
\textbf{third dresser from the left} for a \textbf{Strength Seed}. Keep
it unused for Chapter 5 allocation.

The sisters need armor more urgently than weapons. Nara can contribute
with her starting attack and Heal, while Mara's early value comes from
Blaze and Sap. Selling their starting Silk Robe and Dancer's Costume can
help fund two Leather Dresses, but only do so when the replacements are
purchased immediately.

Train near town until both sisters are approximately level 4. Rest
whenever MP becomes low rather than continuing with only weak physical
attacks. Follow the road north from Monbaraba, pass the sign, and enter
Kievs.

\section{Kievs: Edgar's Home}\label{kievs-edgars-home}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 4 on arrival; 6-7 before the cave\\
\textbf{Inn:} Free\\
\textbf{Fixed pickup:} Lifeforce Nuts

The innkeeper recognizes Edgar's daughters and lets them rest for free,
making Kievs the chapter's best early leveling base. Speak with everyone
to learn that Edgar discovered the Secret of Evolution, attempted to
destroy it, and had another pupil named Orin who may be hiding in the
cave west of town.

Enter Edgar's house in the northeast and search the pots for
\textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}. Save them for the reunited party. The chest in
the basement of the northeast house is empty; the basement otherwise
contains only story context. At night, a friendly Slime appears in town
for optional dialogue.

\subsection{Kievs Shop}\label{kievs-shop}

Raise both sisters to level 6 or 7. Cross the bridge north of Kievs,
travel west, then cross the next bridge south to the cave entrance.

\section{Cave West of Kievs}\label{cave-west-of-kievs}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 6-7\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Wing of Wyvern, Lamp of Darkness, 240G,
Lifeforce Nuts, Sphere of Silence\\
\textbf{Optional recruit:} Orin

The cave is long enough to exhaust an undisciplined spell budget. Nara
should reserve MP for Heal, while Mara uses Blaze or Firebal only
against groups that threaten more damage than basic attacks can manage.
Neither sister is expected to know Outside at the recommended entry
level, so plan to walk back out.

\subsection{Route to the Lamp and
Orin}\label{route-to-the-lamp-and-orin}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Follow the opening passage until a northern side room appears. It
  contains only an NPC, so continue west on the main route.
\item
  At the next branch, go north to the chest containing a \textbf{Wing of
  Wyvern}.
\item
  Return to the main route, continue east, then turn north at the
  apparent dead end and step on the yellow elevator switch.
\item
  On the next floor, walk south, pass west through the columns, circle
  north around the small pool, and enter the western room.
\item
  Use that room's elevator to descend again.
\item
  On the lower floor, walk east to the wall, turn south, and cross the
  long bridge west.
\item
  Open the chest for the \textbf{Lamp of Darkness}, then speak with Orin
  beside it and accept his help.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Edgar's Pupil}

\textbf{Trigger:} Speak with Orin beside the Lamp of Darkness and have
him join the party. The achievement description is ``Get Orin to join
your party.''

\end{achievementalert}

Orin acts independently, cannot receive equipment, and immediately
changes the party's risk profile. Let him handle routine physical
offense while Nara and Mara preserve MP. His strength also enables him
to break locked doors during the coming Keeleon investigation.

\subsection{Route to the Sphere of
Silence}\label{route-to-the-sphere-of-silence}

Return by elevator to the floor with the pool. Walk east from the pool
and turn south. At the fork:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Take the western branch to the chest containing \textbf{240G}.
\item
  Backtrack and take the eastern branch south.
\item
  Enter the room to the east and open the chest for \textbf{Lifeforce
  Nuts}.
\item
  Return to the main path and continue south to the next elevator.
\item
  Follow the short passage on the new floor to another elevator.
\item
  At the bottom, walk north and follow the western path to the lone
  chest containing the \textbf{Sphere of Silence}.
\end{enumerate}

The Sphere is the intended answer to Balzack's spellcasting. Keep it in
Nara or Mara's inventory and do not sell it. Backtrack through the
elevators to the entrance, then use the Wing of Wyvern outside or return
on foot to Kievs.

Enemies}\label{cave-west-of-kievs-enemies}

\section{Return to Monbaraba: Private
Dance}\label{return-to-monbaraba-private-dance}

The \texttt{Private\ Dance} interaction requires a male character in the
party, so Nara and Mara cannot trigger it alone. Orin provides the
earliest opportunity. Return to Monbaraba after recruiting him; walk
back through Kievs or use Return once Mara has learned it.

Make it night with the Lamp of Darkness if necessary, enter Monbaraba's
bar, and speak with the dancing girl. Accept her free private dance and
allow the scene to finish.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Private Dance} -- Needs correction, cannot be
achieved in chapter 4.

\textbf{Trigger:} Accept the Monbaraba dancing girl's Puff-Puff offer
while Orin is accompanying the sisters.

\end{achievementalert}

This detour is easiest now because the Lamp guarantees nighttime and
Orin will leave near the end of the chapter. Rest in Monbaraba or at
Kievs, then travel north and slightly east of Kievs to Keeleon Castle.

\section{Keeleon Castle: First
Investigation}\label{keeleon-castle-first-investigation}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 8\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} None on this visit\\
\textbf{Required companion:} Orin

The castle's doors are locked, but Orin forces them open. Explore both
floors and speak with everyone. The new ruler occupies a throne room
that cannot be reached through any visible doorway, and the Counselor
sits in a room in the southeast corner of 1F.

The important clues are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Balzack has become the ruler associated with Keeleon's experiments.
\item
  The Counselor knows the route to the concealed throne room.
\item
  He panics at loud noises and previously imprisoned someone for
  frightening him.
\end{itemize}

Remember the Counselor's room and the dead-end passage beside it. The
route cannot advance until the sisters obtain a suitable noisemaker.
Leave the castle and walk north to Haville.

\section{Haville: Finding the
Noisemaker}\label{haville-finding-the-noisemaker}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 9-10\\
\textbf{Inn:} 6G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} None

Haville is the chapter's equipment hub and eventual escape port. Speak
with the townspeople to learn that ships leave for Endor. More
importantly, visit the jail and question the prisoner who previously
frightened Keeleon's Counselor. He used a \textbf{Gunpowder Jar} taken
from Aktemto Mine, identifying the next required item.

Upgrade the sisters before traveling west. Nara benefits most from
sustained defense because she must remain alive to heal; Mara needs
enough protection to survive while casting. Orin's fixed equipment
cannot be changed.

The preferred end-of-chapter sets are \textbf{Morning Star / Half Plate
Armor / Scale Shield / Feather Hat} for Nara and \textbf{Venomous Dagger
/ Fur Coat / Feather Hat} for Mara.

Before going to Aktemto, visit the House of Prophecy on the small
peninsula west of Haville. The nun's vision is optional foreshadowing
rather than a progression gate. Leave the shrine and continue northwest
to Aktemto.

\section{Aktemto and the Mine}\label{aktemto-and-the-mine}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 12-13\\
\textbf{Inn:} 8G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Mystic Acorns, Silver Tarot Cards, Gunpowder
Jar\\
\textbf{Exit plan:} Mara's Outside spell

Aktemto is being poisoned by gas from its mine. Speak with the
residents, rest, and save before descending at the north end of town.
Mara should know \textbf{Outside at level 9} and \textbf{Bang at level
11}; Nara should be approaching \textbf{Healmore at level 13}. Preserve
enough MP for healing and one Outside cast.

\subsection{Mine Route}\label{mine-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Enter the mine and immediately walk east to the chest containing
  \textbf{Mystic Acorns}.
\item
  Return to the route north. At the first split, go east and continue
  north to the wall.
\item
  Turn east, then north again. At the next split, go west and north to
  the chest containing the \textbf{Silver Tarot Cards}.
\item
  Backtrack to the split and take the eastern route to the ladder.
\item
  On the next floor, travel north as far as possible, then turn east to
  another ladder.
\item
  On the lowest required floor, speak with the miners and open the
  nearby chest for the \textbf{Gunpowder Jar}.
\end{enumerate}

Do not equip or use the Silver Tarot Cards during the required route.
Their random battle effects are less dependable than Nara's Morning
Star, and they retain value for Chapter 5. Use Outside after securing
the Gunpowder Jar unless the party deliberately wants more experience on
the return climb.

\subsection{Aktemto Region and Mine
Enemies}\label{aktemto-region-and-mine-enemies}

\end{guidetable}

Return to Haville after leaving the mine. Restore HP and MP, verify the
Sphere of Silence and Gunpowder Jar are in the sisters' inventories, and
save before returning south to Keeleon Castle.

\section{Keeleon Castle: Balzack}\label{keeleon-castle-balzack}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 13\\
\textbf{Required items:} Gunpowder Jar, Sphere of Silence\\
\textbf{Recovery:} Haville immediately before entry\\
\textbf{Outcome:} Balzack victory followed by a scripted loss to Keeleon

Enter the castle and go to the southeast corner of 1F. Take the narrow
dead-end passage beside the Counselor's room and use the
\textbf{Gunpowder Jar}. The blast sends the Counselor toward the hidden
throne room.

Follow at a distance. If the sisters get too close, the Counselor stops
rather than revealing the entrance. Remain behind the corner while he
crosses the northern hall and disappears through the wall. Search the
wall where he vanished, press the concealed switch, and enter the throne
room. Heal before speaking with Balzack.

\subsection{Boss: Balzack}\label{boss-balzack}

Have Nara or Mara use the \textbf{Sphere of Silence on the first round}
to block Balzack's spellcasting. It does not stop his physical attacks
or fire breath, so Nara must continue monitoring the entire party. Mara
should cast Sap until Orin and Nara can deal reliable physical damage,
then use Firebal or Bang when healing is covered. Orin attacks
automatically and should provide most of the sustained damage.

Do not conserve MP for after this victory; the following sequence
restores the party before its scripted outcome. Once Balzack's 240 HP
are depleted, the party receives \textbf{500 EXP}.

\subsection{Scripted Battle: Keeleon}\label{scripted-battle-keeleon}

Keeleon appears immediately after Balzack. This battle is intentionally
unwinnable. Do not consume rare items, reset, or grind because the party
is defeated quickly; let the scripted loss proceed. The story continues
in the castle prison rather than producing a normal game over.

\section{Prison Escape and Orin's
Stand}\label{prison-escape-and-orins-stand}

The sisters awaken in a cell beneath Keeleon Castle. Speak with the old
prisoner, then pass through the opening in the wall into the adjoining
escape route. Open the chest for the \textbf{Boarding Pass}.

Follow the passage toward the surface. Guards discover the escape, and
Orin remains behind to hold them off while Nara and Mara flee. He leaves
the party permanently here, so \texttt{Private\ Dance} should already be
complete.

Travel north to Haville. No additional boss blocks the port, but do not
board until the chapter-end inventory is ready.

\section{Haville Departure}\label{haville-departure}

The ship leaves from the northwest port. Before showing the Boarding
Pass, spend remaining gold on equipment or sellable items because loose
Chapter 4 gold will not carry into Chapter 5.

Prioritize:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Nara's Half Plate Armor, Scale Shield, Feather Hat, and Morning Star.
\item
  Mara's Fur Coat, Feather Hat, and Venomous Dagger.
\item
  Extra Half Plate Armor or another high-value item for the Hero's early
  Chapter 5 equipment.
\item
  Medical Herbs only after durable equipment and portable value are
  covered.
\end{enumerate}

Keep the Silver Tarot Cards and unused stat items. The Sphere of Silence
used in this chapter does not remove the need to recover it again during
Chapter 5; that later retrieval will be called out before Esturk's
Palace.

Enter the northwest port and speak to the deckhand, who accepts the
\textbf{Boarding Pass}. Once aboard, talk to every passenger, then speak
with the captain. His confirmation is the point of no return. Agree to
depart for Endor.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Refugees}

\textbf{Trigger:} Set sail from Haville after the Keeleon prison escape
and complete Chapter 4.

\end{achievementalert}

\section{Chapter 4 Completion Check}\label{chapter-4-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Private Dance, Edgar's Pupil, and Refugees
  unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Party levels:} Approximately level 13, with Mara knowing
  Outside and Bang and Nara at or near Healmore.
\item
  \textbf{Nara equipment:} Morning Star / Half Plate Armor / Scale
  Shield / Feather Hat preferred.
\item
  \textbf{Mara equipment:} Venomous Dagger / Fur Coat / Feather Hat
  preferred.
\item
  \textbf{Inventory:} Silver Tarot Cards and unused Mystic Acorns,
  Strength Seed, and Lifeforce Nuts retained.
\item
  \textbf{Story items:} Gunpowder Jar consumed; Boarding Pass used;
  Sphere of Silence must be reacquired in Chapter 5.
\item
  \textbf{Temporary companion:} Orin leaves during the Keeleon escape.
\item
  \textbf{Carried forward:} Nara and Mara's levels and ordinary personal
  inventory.
\item
  \textbf{Not carried forward:} Loose Chapter 4 gold.
\item
  \textbf{Next chapter:} The Hero's journey begins, and the four earlier
  parties start converging.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Chapter 5 Begins: The Hero, Endor, and the
Wagon}\label{chapter-5-begins-the-hero-endor-and-the-wagon}

\section{Section Achievements: Chapter 5
Opening}\label{section-achievements-chapter-5-opening}

Tunnel Rat & 5 & Be the 1000th person to use the new tunnel & Cross
Taloon's completed Endor-Branca tunnel for the first time as the
Hero. \\
Hidden Treasure - Endor & 5 & Find the hidden treasure in Endor & Open
the concealed Lifeforce Nuts chest below the western coliseum approach;

Caravan & 10 & Acquire the wagon & Recover the Symbol of Faith from the

\end{guidetable}

Chapter 5 begins the continuous main quest. The Hero starts alone at
level 1, while the characters developed during Chapters 1-4 retain their
levels and ordinary personal equipment until they rejoin. This opening
section reunites the Hero with Nara and Mara, introduces the tactics
system, and secures the wagon that will eventually carry the larger
cast.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Complete the opening events in the hidden village and salvage its
  remaining items.
\item
  Equip and level the Hero around the Woodman's Shack and Branca.
\item
  Cross Taloon's completed tunnel to Endor and complete the delayed
  \texttt{Tunnel\ Rat} setup.
\item
  Verify Endor's hidden Lifeforce Nuts chest, then recruit Nara and
  Mara.
\item
  Recover any useful Chapter 3 treasure left in the Silver Statuette
  cave or purchase equivalent Hero equipment.
\item
  Return through Branca and meet Hector at the Desert Inn.
\item
  Clear the Cave of Betrayal's solo encounters, recover the Symbol of
  Faith, and acquire the wagon.
\end{enumerate}

\section{The Hero's Hometown}\label{the-heros-hometown}

\textbf{Starting level:} 1\\
\textbf{Starting equipment:} Copper Sword, Basic Clothes\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} Medical Herb, Feather Hat

The chapter opens in the secluded village where the Hero has been raised
and trained. Speak with the Hero's mother to receive a Lunch for the
Hero's father. Explore the village and talk to everyone before going
north to the pond. Give the Lunch to the Hero's father, return home,
speak with the Hero's mother again, and sit down for the meal.

The village is attacked during the resulting sequence. When control
returns in the underground shelter, walk north and search the pots for a
\textbf{Medical Herb}. Climb out and inspect the ruined village. The
former flower patch is now a two-by-two square of sand; search its
center tiles for a hidden \textbf{Feather Hat} associated with Celia.
The Hero cannot equip it, but it sells for 210G and provides most of the
first equipment budget.

No enemy encounter occurs inside the village. Once the Medical Herb and
Feather Hat are accounted for, leave through the southern exit.

\section{Woodman's Shack and Early
Training}\label{woodmans-shack-and-early-training}

Walk a short distance south to the Woodman's Shack. Search all three
pots inside to collect a second \textbf{Medical Herb}, \textbf{50G}, and
\textbf{Leather Armor}. Equip the Leather Armor immediately, then speak
with the woodsman for background about the Hero's origins.

The Hero begins with Expel but no healing spell. Fight only short loops
near the shack and Branca until reaching level 3 or 4; Blaze is learned
at level 3, while Healmore does not arrive until level 5. Use Medical
Herbs before low HP turns an ordinary encounter into a return from
defeat.

Enemies}\label{hometown-and-branca-region-enemies}

\section{Branca and the Completed
Tunnel}\label{branca-and-the-completed-tunnel}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 2 on arrival; 3-4 before leaving\\
\textbf{Inn:} 3G\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} None

Branca is the first full service point. Speak with the King and
townspeople to learn that the Hero must stop the return of the Ruler of
Evil and that two survivors from earlier chapters were last seen in
Endor. Taloon's completed tunnel provides the direct route southwest.

Sell the unusable Feather Hat for 210G. The free Leather Armor is
adequate for the tunnel trip, so prioritize a weapon and shield. A
Boomerang and Scale Shield cost 530G together; buy the Boomerang first
if battles are slow and the shield first if the Hero is consuming too
many herbs. The Wooden Hat is another inexpensive lasting upgrade.

Skip & Club & 30G & +7 attack & Weaker than the starting Copper
Sword. \\
Skip & Copper Sword & 100G & +12 attack & Already equipped. \\
Skip & Thorn Whip & 200G & +14 attack & Too small an improvement. \\
High & Boomerang & 350G & +18 attack & Best Branca weapon and useful
against groups. \\
Skip & Leather Armor & 180G & +12 defense & Already obtained at the
shack. \\
Situational & Chain Mail & 350G & +18 defense & A useful bridge if funds
permit. \\
Skip & Leather Shield & 90G & +4 defense & Save for the Scale Shield
when possible. \\
High & Scale Shield & 180G & +7 defense & Best shield sold in Branca. \\
Skip & Leather Hat & 65G & +2 defense & Save for the Wooden Hat. \\
High & Wooden Hat & 120G & +6 defense & Best helmet sold in Branca. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\end{guidetable}

Save after reaching level 3 or 4. Leave Branca and travel southwest to
the tunnel built by Taloon. The passage is linear. Walk all the way
through and climb the stairs on the Endor side.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Tunnel Rat} -- Needs correction, this will
not trigger until some internal condition is met and the guard stops
you.

\textbf{Trigger:} Complete the Hero's first crossing of the finished
Endor-Branca tunnel. This is the delayed payoff for Taloon's Chapter 3
investment and satisfies ``Be the 1000th person to use the new tunnel.''

\end{achievementalert}

If the achievement does not appear at the first staircase, speak with
the tunnel workers and finish exiting onto the Endor side before
checking again.

\section{Endor: Hidden Treasure and
Reunion}\label{endor-hidden-treasure-and-reunion}

\textbf{Recommended level:} 3-4\\
\textbf{Inn:} 6G per person\\
\textbf{Party gained:} Nara, then Mara\\
\textbf{Hidden treasure:} Lifeforce Nuts

Before recruiting the sisters, resolve the Endor treasure check. Enter
the castle through the western doorway leading toward the coliseum. In
the hallway, take the small opening on the west side to step onto the
castle exterior. Walk south along the outer wall to the concealed
stairwell, descend, and open the lone chest for \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Hidden Treasure - Endor} -- Needs correction.
chest described is wrong, the chest is in the lower right building of
endor and is the cursed sword the multi-edged sword

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the concealed Lifeforce Nuts chest below Endor's
western coliseum approach.

\end{achievementalert}

This chest was available during Chapter 2 and is already collected if
that section was followed. In that case, the chest remains empty and the
achievement should already be unlocked; there is no second early-game
Endor treasure to find. The locked royal-room and hidden-shop treasures
require later keys and are unrelated to this achievement check.

Find Nara just south of the House of Healing on Endor's west side.
Accept her fortune reading. She recognizes the Hero and joins. Enter the
casino beneath the inn and speak with Mara near the slot machines on the
north side; with Nara present, Mara also joins.

Chapter 5 battle control changes here. The Hero is the only permanent
character whose individual action can be selected. Nara and Mara act
according to the chosen tactic and the current battle state. Begin with
\textbf{Normal}, switch to \textbf{Defensive} when healing is falling
behind, and remember that \textbf{Save MP prevents Healus} later in the
game. The sisters retain their Chapter 4 levels and equipment, so they
should immediately stabilize the low-level Hero.

Taloon's former shop is now a vault operated by Neta. Deposit unused
stat seeds, obsolete key items, and valuables that do not need to occupy
combat inventory. Gold placed in the vault is protected from the normal
defeat penalty.

The wedding of Prince Reed and the Princess of Endor can be viewed from
the coliseum for optional story continuity. It is not an achievement or
progression requirement.

\subsection{Endor Shops and Immediate
Priorities}\label{endor-shops-and-immediate-priorities}

Do not begin the \texttt{High\ Roller} grind yet unless the account
already has a substantial Chapter 2 balance. The Hero's equipment and
upcoming solo fights take priority over casino prizes.

\section{Bonmalmo, Lakanaba, and Equipment
Preparation}\label{bonmalmo-lakanaba-and-equipment-preparation}

The next required dungeon separates the Hero from Nara and Mara for two
fixed battles. Use the familiar Endor region to raise the Hero
independently instead of assuming the sisters' Chapter 4 levels will
carry him.

Travel north from Endor to Bonmalmo. The most important purchase is the
\textbf{Broad Sword for 2,000G}, followed by Half Plate Armor, Iron
Shield, and Wooden Hat. If Chapter 3 left the Broad Sword and Half Plate
Armor unopened in the Cave of the Silver Statuette, recover those copies
instead of buying them. The cave's water-level puzzle was already
solved, and the \hyperref[cave-of-the-silver-statuette]{Chapter 3 cave
route} identifies both chests.

\subsection{Chapter 5 Bonmalmo Shops}\label{chapter-5-bonmalmo-shops}

Lakanaba is optional. Its shops have reverted to their ordinary stock,
so do not expect a Sword of Malice stocked during Chapter 3 to remain
available. The useful reason to visit is the field east of town, where
Metal Slimes can appear. Mara's Poison Needle may score an instant
defeat, while ordinary attacks and the Hero's critical hits can also
work. Each Metal Slime is worth 1,350 EXP, but it is likely to flee; do
not make this rare encounter the only leveling plan.

The achievement
\texttt{The\ One\ That\ Didn\textquotesingle{}t\ Get\ Away} requires a
\textbf{King Metal}, not a Metal Slime, so no achievement is expected
here.

Aim for Hero level 7 before leaving the Endor region. Healmore arrives
at level 5 and Return at level 7. The later target is level 10 or 11
before the Cave of Betrayal, where Firebal at level 9 gives the Hero a
dependable group attack.

Return to Endor, cross the completed tunnel back to Branca, and rest and
save. Leave Branca to the east. When the road opens through the
mountains to the south, follow it to the Desert Inn.

\section{Desert Inn and Hector}\label{desert-inn-and-hector}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 7 on arrival; 10-11 before entering the
cave\\
\textbf{Inn:} 6G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickup:} Lunch\\
\textbf{Visible objective:} Primrose and the wagon

The horse and wagon are visible outside, but their owner will not trust
the party yet. Enter the northern house and speak with \textbf{Hector}.
When he asks to be left alone, answer \textbf{No}. He explains that a
former companion betrayed him during an expedition to a cave east of the
inn.

Walk along the grassy northern edge of Hector's room to reach the
northeast chest and take the \textbf{Lunch}. Speak with the other
residents, rest, and save.

Before leaving for the cave, equip the Hero with the best available set:

}

\end{guidetable}

The Hero, not the whole party, must be level 10 or 11. Nara and Mara are
removed for the cave's two important fights, so their equipment cannot
compensate for an underleveled Hero. Carry Medical Herbs even after
learning Healmore; they preserve MP and remain usable if spellcasting
becomes undesirable.

\subsection{Desert Inn Region Enemies}\label{desert-inn-region-enemies}

Train near the inn, where recovery is close, until the Hero reaches the
target. Then travel east across the series of bridges and continue to
the Cave of Betrayal.

\section{Cave of Betrayal}\label{cave-of-betrayal}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 10-11\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Symbol of Faith\\
\textbf{Random encounters:} None inside\\
\textbf{Required spells:} Healmore; Firebal strongly recommended\\
\textbf{Exit restriction:} Outside does not work

The cave contains no random battles. Its danger comes from two mandatory
solo encounters, so there is no way to gain a missing level after
entering. Save at the Desert Inn first.

Walk north from the entrance and force the party through the unusual
wall. Continue along the linear route and break through the next wall.
The floor collapses ahead, separating Nara and Mara from the Hero.

Continue alone and take the stairs down. Walk north until the Hero sees
what appear to be the sisters. Follow them to the dead end, fully heal,
and speak with either one to expose the first pair of impostors.

\begin{landscape}

\subsection{Solo Battle 1: Two
Liclicks}\label{solo-battle-1-two-liclicks}

\chaptermark{Chapter 5 Begins: The Hero, Endor, and the Wagon}

The pair is worth \textbf{56 EXP and 122G}. Begin with physical attacks
while accuracy is intact. If Surround causes repeated misses, use
Firebal to bypass the accuracy penalty and damage the group. Use
Healmore before the Hero falls into range of two consecutive enemy
attacks.

After the fight, the Hero drops to another floor. Follow the apparent
sisters and speak to them when they are found with another pair of
monsters. This group is also an ambush.

\begin{landscape}

\subsection{Solo Battle 2: Tricksy Urchins and Vampire
Bats}\label{solo-battle-2-tricksy-urchins-and-vampire-bats}

\chaptermark{Chapter 5 Begins: The Hero, Endor, and the Wagon}

The group is worth \textbf{922 EXP and 86G}, making it a substantial
catch-up reward for the Hero. Target the Tricksy Urchins first. Firebal
damages their group efficiently; finish survivors with physical attacks.
Keep the Hero above roughly 25-30 HP and cast Healmore before gambling
on another offensive round. Once both Urchins are gone, remove the
Vampire Bats.

\section{Finding the Real Sisters}\label{finding-the-real-sisters}

After the second battle, climb the nearby stairs, follow the passage,
and descend the next staircase. Walk all the way south to find the real
Nara and Mara at the dead end. They ask a question to prove the Hero's
identity: when asked whether \textbf{Nara} was in Endor's casino, answer
\textbf{No}. Mara was the sister at the slot machines. The real sisters
rejoin.

Take the eastern stairs up. Break through the wall while traveling
south, then use the southern stairs to return to 1F. Go west, break
through the wall into the southern room, and descend the central stairs
followed by the next staircase to reach the bottom.

In the lowest chamber, force a path through the cluster of breakable
walls in the center. Open the revealed chest for the \textbf{Symbol of
Faith}. Outside is disabled in this cave, so retrace the physical route
to the entrance.

\section{Acquiring the Wagon}\label{acquiring-the-wagon}

Return west to the Desert Inn and speak with Hector again. He recognizes
the Symbol of Faith as proof that the party remained loyal through the
same trial that destroyed his trust. Hector joins as a temporary
companion and gives the party \textbf{Primrose and the wagon}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Caravan}

\textbf{Trigger:} Show Hector the Symbol of Faith and acquire Primrose
and the wagon.

\end{achievementalert}

The wagon allows additional companions to travel with the active party
once more of the chosen ones are recruited. Hector occupies the fourth
active slot for now and adds physical offense during the upcoming desert
crossing. Rest and save at the inn, but stop here for this section;
Aneaux begins the next route segment.

\section{Chapter 5 Opening Completion
Check}\label{chapter-5-opening-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Tunnel Rat and Caravan unlocked; Hidden
  Treasure - Endor either unlocked now or already completed during
  Chapter 2.
\item
  \textbf{Hero level:} Approximately 10-11.
\item
  \textbf{Hero equipment:} Broad Sword / Half Plate Armor / Iron Shield
  / Wooden Hat preferred.
\item
  \textbf{Permanent party:} Hero / Nara / Mara.
\item
  \textbf{Temporary companion:} Hector.
\item
  \textbf{Travel:} Primrose and the wagon acquired.
\item
  \textbf{Key checks:} Endor's concealed Lifeforce Nuts chest verified;
  any deferred Chapter 3 cave equipment recovered or intentionally left
  behind.
\item
  \textbf{Recovery:} Desert Inn remains the nearest save and rest point.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Aneaux, reached by leaving the desert
  through its southeast corner and traveling west.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Konenber and the Great
Lighthouse}\label{konenber-and-the-great-lighthouse}

\section{Section Achievements: Konenber and the Great
Lighthouse}\label{section-achievements-konenber-and-the-great-lighthouse}

\begin{guidetable}

With the wagon secured, the party can cross the desert and continue
toward the western coast. This section introduces the first Small Medals
in the achievement route, contains the only permanently missable medal
in the set, and ends when Taloon joins with his ship. Do not finish the
Great Lighthouse before collecting both Konenber medals.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Leave the Desert Inn through the southern desert, exit at the
  southeast corner, and visit Aneaux.
\item
  Search Aneaux's graveyard and confirm the story behind the displayed
  Zenithian Armor.
\item
  Reach Konenber and collect both Small Medals, especially the one
  aboard Taloon's unfinished ship.
\item
  Prepare the Hero for the Great Lighthouse and save in Konenber.
\item
  Meet Taloon inside the lighthouse, clear every treasure chest, and
  recover the Fire of Serenity.
\item
  Defeat the Lighthouse Bengal and two Flamers, then restore the beacon.
\item
  Return to Konenber, recruit Taloon, and acquire the ship.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Crossing the Desert to
Aneaux}\label{crossing-the-desert-to-aneaux}

Leave the Desert Inn through its southern exit with the wagon. The
desert's exit is in the southeast corner; mountains block a direct route
farther west. Once outside the sand, travel west to Aneaux.

The enemies here are stronger than those around Branca, but the active
party now has the Hero, Nara, Mara, and Hector. The distance is short,
so use the sisters' MP when a dangerous group appears rather than
carrying unnecessary risk into town.

Enemies}\label{desert-and-aneaux-region-enemies}

\section{Aneaux and the Armor Legend}\label{aneaux-and-the-armor-legend}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 11\\
\textbf{Cheaper inn:} 5G per person\\
\textbf{Expensive inn:} 10G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickup:} Strength Seed

Speak with the guide near the entrance if you want a tour, but do not
pay twice as much for lodging: the cheaper inn provides the same
recovery as its competitor.

Go to the graveyard in the northwest corner. Search the ground directly
in front of the center tombstone for a hidden \textbf{Strength Seed}.
Keep it with the other unused stat items for later allocation.

The temple displays what residents claim is the legendary Zenithian
Armor. Return to the graveyard at night and speak with the ghost of the
warrior Ruvas, who confirms that the displayed armor is not the
original. This conversation is optional, but it establishes the
equipment lead that will matter later.

Leave Aneaux and travel southwest to the port town of Konenber.

\section{Konenber: Two Small Medals}\label{konenber-two-small-medals}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 11 on arrival; 12-13 before the
lighthouse\\
\textbf{Inn:} 6G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} 2 Small Medals\\
\textbf{Permanent missable:} One of the two ship medals; collect both
before completing the lighthouse

Speak with the townspeople to learn that the Great Lighthouse east of
Konenber has gone dark, making the surrounding waters unnavigable.
Taloon owns the new ship under construction and has gone to investigate
the lighthouse himself.

Collect both Small Medals before following him:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Board the old ship at the western port. Go downstairs into its first
  room and search the dresser for \textbf{Small Medal 1}.
\item
  Enter the shipyard building in the northwest, board Taloon's
  unfinished ship, go downstairs, and search the dresser for
  \textbf{Small Medal 2}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 2/32.} One Konenber ship medal becomes
permanently unavailable after the Great Lighthouse mission. The supplied
sources do not identify it consistently, so do not leave Konenber for
the lighthouse until both medals are in inventory.

\end{achievementsetup}

Train in the coastal region or return to the desert for Metal Slime
attempts until the Hero reaches level 12 or 13. Restore the party at the
inn and save. From Konenber, travel east to the shoreline, turn north
into the forest, cross the eastern bridge, then turn south to the Great
Lighthouse.

\section{Great Lighthouse}\label{great-lighthouse}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 12-13\\
\textbf{Fixed rewards:} Luck Seed, 400G, Magic Potion, Strength Seed,
Full Moon Herb, Boomerang, Fire of Serenity, Golden Barrette\\
\textbf{Trap chest:} Man-Eater Chest\\
\textbf{Recovery:} Konenber; plan a separate treasure trip and boss trip
if MP runs low

The encounter rate is high and several enemies use group attacks or
disruptive magic. Do not spend all of Nara and Mara's MP during the
treasure sweep. It is efficient to collect the lower-floor treasure,
return to Konenber, then make a direct fully-rested boss run.

Enter through the large door and walk around the central structure
toward the north stairs. Taloon appears and explains that an evil flame
has corrupted the beacon. The \textbf{Fire of Serenity} elsewhere in the
tower can extinguish it. Taloon then returns to Konenber while the party
completes the dangerous work.

\subsection{Lighthouse Treasure
Checklist}\label{lighthouse-treasure-checklist}

\end{guidetable}

The local RAM-derived treasure records identify the Boomerang as the
right chest and the Man-Eater as the left chest in the 3F western room.
This resolves the left/right disagreement between the supplied
walkthroughs.

\subsection{1F Hidden Room}\label{f-hidden-room}

Before climbing, approach the center structure that resembles a plus
sign. Walk through its north wall from either side to enter the
concealed room. Open the left chest for the \textbf{Luck Seed} and the
right chest for \textbf{400G}. These are the first two
achievement-tracked hidden treasures.

Return to the visible route and climb to 2F.

\subsection{2F Treasure Branches}\label{f-treasure-branches}

Walk south toward the central tile arrangement. Go west down the
corridor, follow it south into the small room, and climb the stairs. On
the upper landing, walk north to the dead-end chest containing a
\textbf{Magic Potion}, then return to 2F.

From the central arrangement, follow the eastern passage to its end and
open the chest for a \textbf{Strength Seed}. Backtrack to the center and
take the stairs directly south of it to resume the ascent.

\subsection{3F Western Room}\label{f-western-room}

Follow the looping passage and enter the eastern opening to speak with
the stranded monster if desired. Return to the route and continue south
until the northern fork. Take the eastern branch first for the
\textbf{Full Moon Herb}, then backtrack and take the western branch
toward the statues.

Before climbing the stairs beside the statues, continue west and follow
the corridor south to two chests. Heal first. The \textbf{right chest
contains a Boomerang}. The \textbf{left chest is a Man-Eater Chest} with
100 HP; it can cripple a character with one attack, so focus the full
party on it and heal immediately after a heavy hit. Defeating it awards
150 EXP and 25G, with a possible Cypress Stick drop.

Return to the statues and climb the stairs.

\subsection{Fire of Serenity and Final Hidden
Treasure}\label{fire-of-serenity-and-final-hidden-treasure}

On 4F, walk directly south and open the chest for the \textbf{Fire of
Serenity}. Return to the stairs and go northeast to the staircase
leading to the summit.

Leave the small entry room on 5F and walk south. Turn west into the
narrow path, follow it north, turn west at the top, then take the
parallel path south. Continue around to the isolated room in the
northwest corner and open the chest for a \textbf{Golden Barrette}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Hidden Treasure - Great Lighthouse}

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the 5F Golden Barrette chest after collecting the
hidden Luck Seed and 400G on 1F. If it does not unlock immediately,
verify every row in the lighthouse treasure checklist before leaving.

\end{achievementalert}

Equip the Golden Barrette on Nara or Mara if either still wears a
Feather Hat. Keep the three stat items unused for later allocation.

\section{Boss: Lighthouse Bengal and Two
Flamers}\label{boss-lighthouse-bengal-and-two-flamers}

After collecting the Golden Barrette, return to the summit's southern
side and approach the monsters circling the corrupted beacon.

\chaptermark{Konenber and the Great Lighthouse}

The encounter awards \textbf{2,220 EXP and 454G}. Focus both Flamers
before attacking the Lighthouse Bengal. The Hero's physical strikes are
the most dependable damage because Mara avoids ineffective fire magic
and Nara may spend turns healing or attempting control spells. Removing
one Flamer quickly cuts the incoming Firebal pressure in half.

Keep the Hero ready to cast Healmore when multiple characters are
injured. If a key character loses a turn to the Bengal's shriek,
continue reducing the remaining Flamer rather than spreading damage.
Once both Flamers fall, the Bengal is a straightforward physical target.

After the battle, stand beside the central beacon and use the
\textbf{Fire of Serenity}. The corrupted flame disappears and the waters
around Konenber become navigable. Cast Outside, then Return to Konenber.

\section{Taloon and the Ship}\label{taloon-and-the-ship}

Find Taloon outside the shipyard in Konenber's northwest corner. Speak
with him and accept him into the party. Taloon brings the personal
inventory prepared at the end of Chapter 3; redistribute useful
equipment and sell portable-value items only as needed.

The completed ship becomes available as soon as Taloon joins.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Starboard the Helm!}

\textbf{Trigger:} Recruit Taloon in Konenber after restoring the Great
Lighthouse and acquire the ship.

\end{achievementalert}

Do not search for the second Konenber medal now: Taloon's ship has left
its construction state, and that medal is no longer obtainable if it was
missed. The old-ship medal remains available, but the completion route
already collected both before the lighthouse.

\section{Before Sailing to Mintos}\label{before-sailing-to-mintos}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Hidden Treasure - Great Lighthouse and
  Starboard the Helm! unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 2/32 collected; both ship medals secured before
  the permanent cutoff.
\item
  \textbf{Hero level:} Approximately 14-15 after the lighthouse boss.
\item
  \textbf{Roster:} Hero, Nara, Mara, and Taloon permanent; Hector
  remains a temporary companion.
\item
  \textbf{Travel:} Wagon and ship available.
\item
  \textbf{Key equipment:} Broad Sword, Half Plate Armor, Iron Shield,
  and Wooden Hat or better on the Hero; Golden Barrettes on Nara and
  Mara as available.
\item
  \textbf{Treasure:} All nine lighthouse chests or encounters resolved,
  including the three hidden achievement targets.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Sail directly south from Konenber, land on
  the southern coast, and walk east to Mintos.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Mintos, Padequia, Keeleon, and
Santeem}\label{mintos-padequia-keeleon-and-santeem}

\section{Section Achievements: Mintos, Padequia, Keeleon, and
Santeem}\label{section-achievements-mintos-padequia-keeleon-and-santeem}

The ship opens the world, but this section follows a deliberate route
rather than scattering the party across every reachable shore. First
cure Cristo and reunite Alena's group. Then recover the Magic Key,
rescue Ragnar from Keeleon, complete the most efficient key-and-medal
detours, and settle the sisters' unfinished fight with Balzack in
Santeem.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Sail from Konenber to Mintos, collect the Treasure Map and two Small
  Medals, and recruit Brey.
\item
  Visit Soretta and clear the Cave of the Padequia Seed.
\item
  Grow the Padequia Root, cure Cristo, and recruit Alena and Cristo.
\item
  Raid Burland's Thief's Key treasury before crossing to the Keeleon
  continent.
\item
  Follow the Magic Key clues through Keeleon, Haville, Kievs, and
  Edgar's hidden laboratory.
\item
  Defeat Keeleon and recruit Ragnar, completing the eight chosen ones.
\item
  Collect the nearby island, Endor, and Seaside Village medals and
  key-gated treasure.
\item
  Return to Santeem, defeat evolved Balzack, and recover the Magma Staff
  and Flute of Uncovering.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Sailing to Mintos}\label{sailing-to-mintos}

Board the ship at Konenber and sail directly south until reaching the
next continent. Land on its northern coast and walk east to Mintos.
Ocean encounters are generally less frequent than land encounters, but
the ship can be attacked anywhere outside a town or dungeon.

\section{Mintos: Map, Medal, and a Sick
Companion}\label{mintos-map-medal-and-a-sick-companion}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 14-15\\
\textbf{Inn:} 9G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} Small Medal, Treasure Map\\
\textbf{Recruit:} Brey

Search the ground immediately west of the well near the center of town
for \textbf{Small Medal 3}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 3/32.} This is the first medal collected after
acquiring the ship.

\end{achievementsetup}

East of the well, Howden lectures a small audience. Speak with him and
answer \textbf{No} when he asks whether he is a master of the sea. His
follow-up exchange awards the \textbf{Treasure Map}, which shows the
party's world position and marks a later destination.

Enter the large inn and go to the second floor. Cristo is bedridden,
Brey is watching over him, and Alena has already left to search for a
cure. Speak with Brey and accept him into the traveling party. The local
leads identify the Padequia, a medicinal plant once grown in Soretta to
the southeast.

The priority is not to equip every reserve character equally. Give the
active four enough durability for the Padequia cave, then preserve gold
for later equipment. A practical party is Hero / Nara / Mara / Taloon;
Brey can replace Taloon if his levels need attention, but several cave
enemies resist ice magic.

\section{Shrine East of Mintos}\label{shrine-east-of-mintos}

Leave Mintos and travel east to the small forest shrine. Its two travel
doors are not usable for the current route, but the grounds contain a
medal. Follow the grassy outer edge behind the building, enter the
narrow strip between the walls, walk to the dead end, and search for
\textbf{Small Medal 4}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 4/32.} Return to the front of the shrine after
collecting it; no travel door is needed yet.

\end{achievementsetup}

Continue east to the mountain range, turn south along its edge, and
enter Soretta just beyond the mountains.

\section{Soretta and the Lost Padequia
Crop}\label{soretta-and-the-lost-padequia-crop}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 15\\
\textbf{Inn:} 12G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} None before the cave

Soretta's Padequia crop died in a drought years ago. The King now works
in the southeastern field. Speak with him to learn that a former king
preserved one Padequia Seed in a cave southwest of the kingdom.

Soretta has no weapon or armor shop. Its item shop sells Medical Herbs
for 8G, Antidote Herbs for 10G, and Full Moon Herbs for 30G. Rest, save,
and arrange a four-person cave party. Hero / Nara / Mara / Taloon
provides healing, fire offense, Sap, and physical damage; use Brey
instead of Taloon only if leveling him is worth carrying ice magic into
a dungeon containing Iceloths.

Travel southwest to the cave at the end of the peninsula.

\section{Cave of the Padequia Seed}\label{cave-of-the-padequia-seed}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 15-16\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Agility Seed, Mystic Acorns, Robe of Serenity,
800G, Padequia Seed\\
\textbf{Trap chest:} Man-Eater Chest\\
\textbf{Wagon restriction:} Only the four active characters enter

Directional floor tiles carry the party in the arrow's direction until
the route releases them. Plan from the destination of each strip rather
than trying to walk against it. The cave is not especially long, but
repeated wrong turns can consume substantial healing MP.

\subsection{B1: Agility Seed}\label{b1-agility-seed}

Follow the entrance corridor to the first arrow-tile area. Ignore the
first strip on the left. Approach the isolated chest from below by
taking the lower arrow route upward, then open it for an \textbf{Agility
Seed}. Use the arrows to the right to return to the main passage,
continue southwest, and descend the stairs.

\subsection{B2: Alena and the Treasure
Loop}\label{b2-alena-and-the-treasure-loop}

Alena is walking with the adventurers first seen leaving Branca. She
refuses to abandon her own search, so she does not join yet.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Go to the northwest corner and find the two adjacent arrow columns
  pointing north.
\item
  Take the \textbf{left column}, then continue north along the wall to
  the chest containing \textbf{Mystic Acorns}.
\item
  Use the arrows to the right, regain control in the northeast area, and
  move southeast to the two columns pointing south.
\item
  Take the \textbf{left southbound column}. After it releases the party,
  walk west and take the left arrow strip there.
\item
  Walk north past the ice formations, then turn west before stepping
  onto the arrow row along the north wall.
\item
  Enter the large door below and open the chest for the \textbf{Robe of
  Serenity}. Mara or Brey can use this +33 Defense armor; equip it on
  whichever one is active and least protected.
\item
  Leave the room and walk directly east across the two usable tiles to
  reach the northeast chest containing \textbf{800G}.
\item
  Use the adjacent arrows to return southeast. Take the left southbound
  column again, then move west.
\item
  After the arrows release the party, walk south and then east below the
  tile field. Avoid stepping onto the upper arrows while following the
  narrow path to the stairs.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{B3: Padequia Seed}\label{b3-padequia-seed}

The bottom floor compresses the puzzle into one dense arrow field.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Walk north and step onto the north-pointing tile in the northeast
  corner. It carries the party to the northwest section.
\item
  Move five steps west, then step south onto the downward arrow strip.
\item
  From the landing near the southern side, approach the nearby chest
  first if completing every encounter. It is a \textbf{Man-Eater Chest},
  not treasure.
\item
  From the trap chest, walk four steps north and step east onto the
  arrow tile. It deposits the party beside the central chest.
\item
  Open the central chest for the \textbf{Padequia Seed}.
\end{enumerate}

Use Outside and Return to reach Soretta. If the active caster cannot
leave, retrace the arrow route and stairs manually.

\section{Growing the Root and Saving
Cristo}\label{growing-the-root-and-saving-cristo}

Return to the King in Soretta's southeastern field with the Padequia
Seed. He plants it immediately and gives the party the resulting
\textbf{Padequia Root}.

Return to Mintos and go upstairs in the inn. Alena is now beside Cristo.
Speak with her, stand beside Cristo's bed, and use the Padequia Root
from the item menu. Cristo recovers, and both Alena and Cristo join the
party. The Thief's Key returns with Alena.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Miracle Plant}

\textbf{Trigger:} Use the Padequia Root on Cristo in the Mintos inn and
complete his recovery.

\end{achievementalert}

Hector leaves when Alena and Cristo join, but Primrose and the wagon
remain. The permanent roster now includes the Hero, Nara, Mara, Taloon,
Brey, Alena, and Cristo; only Ragnar is still missing.

\section{After Cristo Recovers}\label{after-cristo-recovers}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievement:} Miracle Plant unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 4/32.
\item
  \textbf{Roster:} Seven permanent characters recruited; Hector
  departed.
\item
  \textbf{Key item:} Thief's Key restored through Alena.
\item
  \textbf{Equipment:} Robe of Serenity assigned to Mara or Brey; Full
  Plate Armor and Iron Helmets purchased as needed.
\item
  \textbf{Next objective:} Use the Thief's Key for Burland's treasury,
  then follow Ragnar's trail to Keeleon and recover the Magic Key.
\end{itemize}

\section{Optional Detour: Burland
Treasury}\label{optional-detour-burland-treasury}

With Alena back, the party again carries the Thief's Key. Burland's
first-floor treasury is now worth clearing before the Keeleon route.

Cast Return to Endor, board the ship, follow the river south to the
ocean, then sail west around the cape and north through the channel
between the travel-door shrines. Continue north past Tempe and Lakanaba,
turn east near the Cave North of Lakanaba, and enter the narrow river
leading southeast to Burland. The Chapter 5 monsters around Burland are
much stronger than the Chapter 1 enemies; run from combinations that
threaten the current party.

Inside Burland Castle, go to the northwest corner of 1F and open the
locked door with the Thief's Key. Cross the damaging blue floor to six
chests containing:

\begin{guidetable}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter

1 & Lifeforce Nuts & Save for deliberate endgame allocation. \\
2 & 160G & Current equipment fund. \\
3 & Iron Helmet & Equip on the Hero or reserve for Ragnar. \\
4 & Agility Seed & Save for later allocation. \\
5 & Strength Seed & Save for later allocation. \\
6 & 320G & Current equipment fund. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\end{guidetable}

The floor damage is severe. Cast Healmore after every few steps rather
than waiting for the entire party to reach critical HP. Once all six
chests are open, leave the treasury and return to Mintos.

\section{Following Ragnar to Keeleon}\label{following-ragnar-to-keeleon}

After Cristo recovers, a traveler outside the inn room reports that a
soldier seeking the Hero headed toward Keeleon. Sail directly west from
Mintos until reaching the familiar continent, then land near Keeleon
Castle on its eastern side.

Healie, now human, waits near the western side of the courtyard. Speak
with him to learn that Ragnar was captured while confronting Keeleon and
that the castle now requires a Magic Key. Continue north to Haville and
speak with the weapon-shop owner, who connects Magic Keys with Edgar's
alchemy. Travel south to Kievs and question the residents near Edgar's
house; they identify a hidden laboratory in the Cave West of Kievs.

The traveling merchant inside the Kievs inn now sells late-game
equipment:

Rest for free in Kievs and enter the Cave West of Kievs.

\section{Edgar's Hidden Laboratory and the Magic
Key}\label{edgars-hidden-laboratory-and-the-magic-key}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 17-18 on arrival; 18-19 before
Keeleon\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} Small Medal, Magic Key\\
\textbf{Required key:} Thief's Key

Follow the \hyperref[cave-west-of-kievs]{Chapter 4 cave route} back to
the lower chamber where the Lamp of Darkness was found beside Orin.
Search the now-empty treasure chest. A button in its bottom reveals a
nearby staircase. Descend into Edgar's hidden laboratory.

Search the \textbf{left pot along the north wall} for \textbf{Small
Medal 5}, then open the laboratory chest for the \textbf{Magic Key}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 5/32.} Keep the medal in the shared inventory or
vault; turning medals in later does not invalidate the route count.

\end{achievementsetup}

The Magic Key opens red doors as well as the lower-tier doors already
handled by the Thief's Key, so it supersedes Alena's earlier key.

Use the cave encounters or the area around Kievs to bring the Hero to
level 18 or 19. Phantom Messengers should be priority targets because
they cast Beat and Sap, summon Phantom Knights, resist most magic, and
are immune to fire. Mystic Dolls cast Chaos in groups and share strong
magic resistance; use physical attackers and change tactics if
spellcasters keep selecting ineffective attacks.

\section{Boss: Keeleon Rematch}\label{boss-keeleon-rematch}

Return to Keeleon Castle and enter through the easternmost door with the
Magic Key. Follow the corridor north toward the hidden throne-room
route. Ragnar is being escorted by guards; speak with him to begin the
confrontation. He handles the guards while the active party fights
Keeleon.

Cristo can reduce Keeleon's offense with StopSpell and Surround when the
AI selects them successfully, while Nara is a more healing-focused
alternative. Mara's Blazemore can deal roughly 80 damage when it lands.
Keep the Hero ready to heal because one accurate physical hit followed
by a possible second action can defeat a low-HP caster before the AI
reacts.

Do not spread the party's effort across unnecessary buffs. Maintain
Normal tactics, let Alena and Mara press damage, and use the Hero's turn
for Healmore whenever a possible two-action round could defeat someone.
Keeleon falls after 400 damage and awards 5,100 EXP plus Full Plate
Armor.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Rematch}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Keeleon in the Chapter 5 throne-room battle.

\end{achievementalert}

Ragnar joins immediately after the victory, completing the eight
permanent chosen ones. Give him Keeleon's Full Plate Armor, then obtain
a Battle Axe and Iron Mask in Kievs and an Iron Shield in Endor as funds
allow. Ragnar's Chapter 1 Sword of Malice remains usable until the
Battle Axe is affordable.

\section{Magic Key and Medal Sweep}\label{magic-key-and-medal-sweep}

Complete these stops before sailing to Santeem. They turn the newly
acquired key into immediate equipment and advance the medal route from
5/32 to 7/32.

\subsection{Island Shack North of
Haville}\label{island-shack-north-of-haville}

Sail directly north from Haville to the tiny island with a green center.
Enter the old man's shack and search the \textbf{right pot} for
\textbf{Small Medal 6}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 6/32.} The old man also provides background about
the buried Ruler of Evil.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{Endor's Magic Key Treasure}\label{endors-magic-key-treasure}

Return to Endor. Open the Magic Key door on the southeast building to
reach the hidden armor shop.

The two chests visible below the shop require a route through the
castle. Enter Endor Castle, walk behind the central 1F staircase, face
the middle section of the dark rear wall, and use the Magic Key. Follow
the concealed stairs to the roof, step off onto the shop, and descend to
the chests. Take the \textbf{Strength Seed} and \textbf{Multi-Edge
Sword}. The sword is cursed and damages its wielder in proportion to
damage dealt; store it in the vault rather than equipping it.

Return at night. Enter the castle without touching the pacing guard,
climb through the throne room to the royal bedroom, and open its Magic
Key door. Search the far-right dresser for \textbf{Small Medal 7}, then
open the two chests for the \textbf{Pink Leotard} and \textbf{Feather
Hat}. Give the Pink Leotard to Alena if it improves her current armor.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 7/32.} Endor's earlier hidden-treasure
achievement is separate from this night-only royal-room medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

The casino is now a practical option, but it is not required for the
current bosses. Continue deferring the full \texttt{High\ Roller} grind
unless the player wants a Meteorite Armband or Metal Babble Shield
immediately. Story progress does not close the casino.

\section{Optional Preparation: Seaside
Village}\label{optional-preparation-seaside-village}

This detour secures a medal and the key item for the later Cascade Cave
side route. From Endor, sail south down the river, turn west in the
ocean, and follow the large continent to the village on its eastern
coast. Seaside Village is not added to the Return list, so complete both
searches during the same visit.

Search the ground directly below the central statue for \textbf{Small
Medal 8}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 8/32.} No medals from this section remain
outstanding.

\end{achievementsetup}

At night, walk to the southern beach near the inn and identify the
single tile left dry while the tide covers the surrounding sand.
Remember that square. Return during the day and search the same tile for
the \textbf{Stone of Drought}. It will open Cascade Cave after the Final
Key is obtained.

\section{Return to Santeem}\label{return-to-santeem}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 22-23\\
\textbf{Recovery:} Rest and save in Surene\\
\textbf{Fixed post-boss treasure:} Strength Seed, Flute of Uncovering,
Magma Staff

From Seaside Village, return to the ship and sail east into open water.
Follow the continent's eastern coastline north, then angle northeast
after passing it. Birdsong Tower marks the correct region; Surene and
Santeem lie north of the tower.

Rest in Surene and use the Magic Key on the northeast building's locked
door to reach its upper shop before the boss.

Save, then form the active party \textbf{Hero / Alena / Mara / Ragnar}.
Alena and Ragnar provide durable physical damage, Mara supplies
Blazemore, and the Hero covers healing. Enter Santeem Castle and go
directly to the throne room; talking to roaming monsters can trigger
unnecessary Minidemon battles.

\end{guidetable}

The NES monster data identifies this form as acting twice. Snowstorm is
the central threat, and two Snowstorms in one round can overwhelm a
weakened party. The Hero can attack on the first safe round, then use
Healmore whenever a weaker character cannot comfortably absorb another
Snowstorm. Alena and Ragnar should continue attacking while Mara uses
Blazemore.

Do not use FendSpell indiscriminately. A protected character cannot
receive healing magic from another party member, which can make group
recovery harder. If the chosen party cannot stabilize after a heavy
opening, switch Mara for Nara or Cristo and use a more defensive tactic,
accepting a slower fight in exchange for additional healing.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: You've Changed}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Balzack's evolved form in the Santeem throne
room.

\end{achievementalert}

\section{Santeem Treasure and Immediate
Cleanup}\label{santeem-treasure-and-immediate-cleanup}

After the victory, the flame blocking the northeast treasure room on 1F
disappears. Use the Magic Key and StepGuard to cross the damaging floor,
then open the three chests:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Strength Seed}
\item
  \textbf{Flute of Uncovering}
\item
  \textbf{Magma Staff}
\end{enumerate}

The compact source list also names Mystic Acorns here, but neither its
walkthrough prose nor the RAM-derived fixed-treasure records support a
fourth post-Balzack chest. The verified route therefore tracks only
these three rewards. Keep the Flute and Magma Staff; both are required
later.

Return to Surene and revisit the hidden shop if needed. Exit south from
that shop, follow the narrow grass north behind the main building, then
walk west to the concealed sign. It points the party toward Zenithia and
Stancia.

As an optional final cleanup, visit Frenor at night. Search the sandy
ground where the House of Healing priest stands during the day for
\textbf{Mystic Acorns}. This is separate from the Medical Herb recovered
there after May's rescue in Chapter 2.

\section{Before Sailing to Stancia}\label{before-sailing-to-stancia}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Miracle Plant, Rematch, and You've Changed
  unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 8/32 collected.
\item
  \textbf{Permanent roster:} Hero, Ragnar, Alena, Cristo, Brey, Taloon,
  Nara, and Mara all reunited.
\item
  \textbf{Travel:} Ship and wagon available; Hector has departed.
\item
  \textbf{Keys:} Thief's Key and Magic Key available.
\item
  \textbf{Required future items:} Magma Staff, Flute of Uncovering, and
  Stone of Drought retained.
\item
  \textbf{Equipment:} Full Plate Armor from Keeleon assigned; Ragnar
  upgraded with Battle Axe, Iron Shield, and Iron Mask as funds allow.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Stancia, reached through the waterways of
  the northwestern island.
\end{itemize}

\chapter{Stancia and the Zenithian
Helm}\label{stancia-and-the-zenithian-helm}

\section{Section Achievement: Stancia and the Zenithian
Helm}\label{section-achievement-stancia-and-the-zenithian-helm}

The sign behind Surene points toward Stancia, an island kingdom whose
royal family holds the first piece of equipment required to enter
Zenithia. No dungeon blocks this prize. The challenge is learning the
King's condition, finding the one performer who can answer it, and
arranging the temporary companion correctly before the second audience.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Sail to Stancia's northwestern island and enter through its eastern
  river.
\item
  Use the town boat to collect two Small Medals and inspect the day and
  night shops.
\item
  Enter the throne-room line and let the current party fail the King's
  challenge.
\item
  Return to Monbaraba and recruit the jester Panon from the theater.
\item
  Place Panon first in the active party and speak with the King again.
\item
  Receive and equip the Zenithian Helm, then leave Stancia so Panon
  departs.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Sailing to Stancia}\label{sailing-to-stancia}

From Santeem Castle, board the ship and sail west. Turn north around the
coastline near Tempe, then continue into the northern sea until the
rocky circular island appears. The Treasure Map can confirm the party's
position.

Stancia Castle sits behind waterways that prevent a direct landing.
Enter the river from the island's eastern side, land the ship southeast
of the castle, then walk north and around the outer terrain to reach the
town entrance.

The trip from the landing point is short. Do not remain outside solely
to grind unless the Hero is below the previous section's level target;
Stancia provides an inn and the route contains no boss.

\section{Navigating Stancia}\label{navigating-stancia}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 24\\
\textbf{Inn:} 7G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} 2 Small Medals\\
\textbf{Key reward:} Zenithian Helm

Speak with the boatman at the entrance to receive the small town boat.
Stancia's streets are mostly waterways, so use this boat to reach its
homes, shops, and castle entrances.

Collect both medals before addressing the King:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Sail to the house in the northeast part of town, go upstairs, and
  search the pots for \textbf{Small Medal 9}.
\item
  During the day, enter Stancia Castle through either side entrance.
  Explore the eastern side of 1F and search the dresser in the far-right
  room for \textbf{Small Medal 10}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 10/32.} Both Stancia medals remain accessible
after the Helm event, but collecting them now keeps the route count
synchronized.

\end{achievementsetup}

\section{Stancia Shops}\label{stancia-shops}

Stancia's first weapon merchant operates from a boat in the northwest
waterways. A second weapon shop opens only at night in the southwest.
The Sword of Lethargy is the only major new offensive option, but the
Helm route does not require buying it.

\section{The King's Challenge}\label{the-kings-challenge}

Use the town boat to enter Stancia Castle through the central entrance.
Climb the first stairs, walk south, then climb again to the throne room.
Several performers are waiting in line because the King has promised any
reward to the person who can make him laugh.

Join the line and wait until the party reaches the front. Speak with the
King. The Hero attempts the challenge and fails. This first audience
establishes that ordinary party members cannot solve the problem and
unlocks the route's performer clue.

Talk with the castle and town residents after the failed attempt. They
mention a talented jester named Panon. The only established performance
town is Monbaraba, so leave Stancia and cast Return there.

\section{Recruiting Panon in
Monbaraba}\label{recruiting-panon-in-monbaraba}

Enter Monbaraba's theater during the day. Go downstairs and continue to
the rear room behind the stage. Panon is waiting there. Speak with him
and accept his offer to join.

Panon is a temporary companion with fixed equipment and unpredictable
actions. No combat is required for this route, so his battle value does
not matter. Return to Stancia immediately.

Before entering the castle, use the Member command to place Panon in the
active party, then move him into the \textbf{first party position}.
Merely carrying him in the wagon or placing him behind the Hero does not
satisfy the audience setup described by the supplied walkthrough.

\section{Receiving the Zenithian
Helm}\label{receiving-the-zenithian-helm}

Return to the throne room with Panon leading and speak with the King.
Panon does not perform a conventional joke. Instead, he confronts the
King about using the contest to distract a frightened kingdom,
persuading him to entrust the royal heirloom to the chosen ones.

The King gives the party the \textbf{Zenithian Helm}. Equip it on the
Hero immediately for +30 Defense.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Not So Funny}

\textbf{Trigger:} Receive the Zenithian Helm from the King of Stancia
after presenting Panon as party leader.

\end{achievementalert}

Leave Stancia by foot or cast Return. Panon departs once the party
leaves, freeing the temporary companion slot. The Helm remains equipped
on the Hero and is the first of the four Zenithian equipment pieces
collected by this route.

\section{Stancia Completion Check}\label{stancia-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievement:} Not So Funny unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 10/32 collected.
\item
  \textbf{Zenithian equipment:} Helm 1/4; Shield, Armor, and Sword
  remain.
\item
  \textbf{Hero equipment:} Zenithian Helm equipped for +30 Defense.
\item
  \textbf{Temporary companion:} Panon recruited, presented to the King,
  and released after leaving Stancia.
\item
  \textbf{Missable checks:} Both Stancia medals collected before
  departure.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Burland, where the King can explain what
  happened to the Zenithian Shield.
\end{itemize}

\chapter{Gardenbur and the Zenithian
Shield}\label{gardenbur-and-the-zenithian-shield}

Shield}\label{section-achievements-gardenbur-and-the-zenithian-shield}
