The Zenithian Helm points the party toward the remaining three pieces.
Stancia's residents identify Burland as the last known home of the
Zenithian Shield, but that lead ultimately sends the party to the
isolated kingdom of Gardenbur. This section also establishes the
Rosaville story lead, converts the first ten Small Medals into useful
equipment, and earns the Final Key.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Ask the King of Burland about the Zenithian Shield.
\item
  Sleep at Izmit to witness the dream involving Rosa, Saro, and a hidden
  staircase.
\item
  Visit the Small Medal King and spend the first ten routed medals.
\item
  Use the Magma Staff to open the mountain passage into Gardenbur.
\item
  Collect Gardenbur's Small Medal, Agility Seed, and hidden Fire Claw.
\item
  Trigger the Bronze Amulet accusation and leave one reserve character
  as collateral.
\item
  Clear the Cave Southeast of Gardenbur and defeat Bakor.
\item
  Receive the Final Key, release the hostage, and claim the Zenithian
  Shield.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Burland's Shield Lead}\label{burlands-shield-lead}

Cast Return to Burland. If Burland is not yet in the Return list, sail
to the river between Burland and Izmit and land on the castle side.
Speak with the King. He explains that a former king gave the Zenithian
Shield to the Queen of Gardenbur roughly a century ago. The Counselor
adds that volcanic activity isolated Gardenbur behind mountains.

The Magma Staff recovered at Santeem is the answer to that barrier. Keep
it in an active character's inventory rather than the vault.

Chapter 5 encounters in this region are far stronger than Ragnar's
original opposition:

\section{Izmit's Shared Dream}\label{izmits-shared-dream}

Travel north to Izmit and stay at the inn. The party shares a dream of
the elf Rosa imprisoned at the top of a tower. Saro visits her, plays a
distinct melody that reveals a staircase at the tower's base, and speaks
of destroying humanity. Rosa calls for help as the dream ends.

This is setup rather than an achievement trigger. Remember both the
tower layout and Saro's melody: the Flute of Uncovering found in Santeem
reproduces it. Rosaville will be handled after Gardenbur.

\section{Small Medal King}\label{small-medal-king}

\textbf{Inn:} 10G per person\\
\textbf{Small Medals found so far:} 10/32\\
\textbf{House of Healing:} None

From the Burland and Izmit river, sail northwest to the ocean. Turn east
and follow the continent's northern coastline until it curves south. A
tiny island shrine lies just south of a cave embedded in the claw-shaped
coast. Enter the shrine to meet the Small Medal King.

With ten medals, buy one \textbf{Sword of Miracles} and one \textbf{Hat
of Happiness}. The guide's running total tracks medals found, including
those exchanged, so it remains 10/32 after these purchases. If
conserving all medals for a different prize order, store them in the
Endor vault rather than occupying active inventory.

Speak with the knight at the inn for an early clue that the Zenithian
Armor lies in the Shrine of Breaking Waves. That dungeon belongs to the
next route section.

\section{Opening the Road to
Gardenbur}\label{opening-the-road-to-gardenbur}

Return to Burland and board the ship. Sail east through the river until
reaching land, disembark, then travel south around the lake and continue
southeast. A narrow trail turns north toward a visibly thin section of
mountain wall.

Stand directly south of that single mountain tile and use the
\textbf{Magma Staff}. The eruption removes the obstruction and opens the
path east to Gardenbur. The change is permanent; the staff remains in
inventory for its combat use and does not need to be used again here.

\section{Gardenbur Castle}\label{gardenbur-castle}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 24-25\\
\textbf{Inn:} 8G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups before the accusation:} Small Medal, Fire Claw\\
\textbf{Fixed pickup after the audience:} Agility Seed

Gardenbur's only shop sells armor. Make any purchases before triggering
the theft accusation so the party is fully prepared before a member is
detained.

\subsection{Small Medal 11}\label{small-medal-11}

Go to the kitchen on the west side of 1F and search the pots for
\textbf{Small Medal 11}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 11/32.} Collect this pot before leaving for the
thief's cave.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{Hidden Fire Claw}\label{hidden-fire-claw}

Approach the central staircase on 1F and climb to 2F. Immediately walk
back down the same stairs. Once Alena returns to 1F, walk north through
the wall directly behind the staircase to reveal the concealed passage.
Follow it to the hidden basement and cross the damaging floor to the
chest containing the \textbf{Fire Claw}.

Equip the Fire Claw on Alena. It provides +60 Attack and can be used as
an item in battle for a fire effect, although her later Stilleto
Earrings will usually produce better damage through two attacks and more
critical-hit opportunities.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Hidden Treasure - Gardenbur}

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the concealed Fire Claw chest behind Gardenbur
Castle's central staircase.

\end{achievementalert}

\subsection{The Bronze Amulet
Accusation}\label{the-bronze-amulet-accusation}

On 1F, enter the small room just north of the armor shop and speak with
the woman inside. She tells the party to inspect the dresser, then
leaves. Search the drawers; they are empty. A nun enters and accuses the
party of stealing the Bronze Amulet.

After the jail scene, wait for the guard to return and escort the party
to the Queen. Maintain innocence during the audience. The Queen agrees
to release the party on the condition that Bakor, the actual thief, is
captured, but she keeps one permanent companion as collateral.

Leave a reserve who is not needed in the planned cave party.
\textbf{Taloon} is the simplest choice if the active team is Hero /
Alena / Mara / Cristo. The detained companion receives no cave
experience and cannot be accessed through the wagon until released.

Before leaving the throne-room area, enter the small room immediately
east of the Queen and search its dresser for an \textbf{Agility Seed}.
Rest, save, and travel southeast to the thief's cave.

\section{Cave Southeast of Gardenbur}\label{cave-southeast-of-gardenbur}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 25\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Small Medal, Agility Seed, 1,200G, Strength
Seed, Iron Mask, Dragon Shield\\
\textbf{Boss reward:} Agility Seed\\
\textbf{Recommended active party:} Hero / Alena / Mara / Cristo

This cave has a dense network of ladders and looping ledges. Keep Mara
for Blazemore and Cristo for healing and StopSpell; Alena supplies
consistent physical damage. If Cristo repeatedly selects ineffective
instant-death magic, Nara is a safer healing substitute.

\subsection{B1 Treasure Route}\label{b1-treasure-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  From the entrance, walk all the way south, then turn west and follow
  the outer path north.
\item
  Descend the ladder and continue south to the intersection.
\item
  Take the narrow eastern path to its end and open the chest for
  \textbf{Small Medal 12}.
\item
  Return west to the wall, then walk north and follow the path as it
  turns east.
\item
  Take the narrow southern passage. Follow its long loop south and
  around, ignoring the first two ladders, then approach the central
  platform from the north and open the chest for an \textbf{Agility
  Seed}.
\item
  Backtrack to the two ladders, go north, and climb onto the raised
  platform.
\item
  Follow the linear path east. Ignore the first narrow passage leading
  south and continue to the eastern ladder.
\item
  Descend and continue south to the next ladder, then climb to the chest
  containing \textbf{1,200G}.
\item
  Backtrack to the narrow southern passage that was skipped, follow it
  to the sandy staircase, and descend to B2.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 12/32.} The cave medal is the only new medal in
the Gardenbur route.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{B2 Equipment Route}\label{b2-equipment-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Walk south and take the left ladder.
\item
  Turn west, take the narrow path south, climb two ladders, and walk
  north to the \textbf{Strength Seed} chest.
\item
  Return south and turn east. Continue to the two ladders leading south,
  descend them, and keep walking south.
\item
  At the western side path beside the next ladder, climb onto the raised
  platform. Follow it north, west, and north up another ladder.
\item
  Turn west, descend, then walk north to the chest on the west-wall
  platform containing an \textbf{Iron Mask}.
\item
  Continue to the southwest corner of B2 and open the chest containing
  the \textbf{Dragon Shield}.
\item
  Retrace the route to the east side. In the southeast corner, climb the
  ladder and follow the linear western passage to the stairs down to B3.
\end{enumerate}

Equip the Dragon Shield on the Hero or Ragnar; its +30 Defense and
breath protection make it a major upgrade. The Iron Mask provides +25
Defense to the other front-line fighter.

\subsection{Gardenbur Cave Enemies}\label{gardenbur-cave-enemies}

\section{Boss: Bakor}\label{boss-bakor}

The containers on B3 are empty. Restore the party before speaking with
the sleeping thief. Bakor runs around the chamber when confronted; catch
and speak with him to begin the fight.

Normal tactics let Cristo choose healing or StopSpell while Mara
attacks. If StopSpell lands before Bakor casts Increase, the physical
fighters can end the battle efficiently. A Sword of Lethargy on the Hero
can also put Bakor to sleep and deny turns.

If Increase raises Bakor's Defense, stop wasting physical turns. The
Hero should cast Zap while Mara uses Blazemore; Alena can continue
attacking for critical-hit chances. Keep enough HP to survive Snowstorm
followed by a physical hit. Defeating Bakor awards 7,400 EXP and an
\textbf{Agility Seed}, and a Gardenbur soldier takes him into custody.

Use Outside and return to Gardenbur Castle.

\section{Final Key and Zenithian
Shield}\label{final-key-and-zenithian-shield}

Speak with the Queen after Bakor's capture. She clears the party's name
and gives the Hero the \textbf{Final Key}, which opens every door and
supersedes the Magic Key.

Go to the jail and release the companion held as collateral. From the
stairs leading to the jail, walk directly east to the other staircase
and descend into the locked basement. Use the Final Key, cross the
damaging floor with StepGuard, and open the lone chest for the
\textbf{Zenithian Shield}. Equip it on the Hero for +55 Defense.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Proof of Innocence}

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the locked Gardenbur basement chest and obtain
the Zenithian Shield after receiving the Final Key.

\end{achievementalert}

The Queen recommends Rosaville to the south. Do not begin that route
yet: the next section starts with the newly available Final Key treasure
sweep, then continues to Rosaville and the remaining Zenithian equipment
leads.

\section{Gardenbur Completion Check}\label{gardenbur-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Hidden Treasure - Gardenbur and Proof of
  Innocence unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 12/32 found; exchanged medals remain included
  in this cumulative route count.
\item
  \textbf{Zenithian equipment:} Helm and Shield obtained, 2/4.
\item
  \textbf{Keys:} Final Key obtained; it supersedes the earlier keys.
\item
  \textbf{Unique equipment:} Fire Claw secured for Alena; Dragon Shield
  and Iron Mask collected from the cave.
\item
  \textbf{Party:} Hostage released and full permanent roster available
  again.
\item
  \textbf{Required future items:} Magma Staff, Flute of Uncovering, and
  Stone of Drought retained.
\item
  \textbf{Next route:} Final Key treasure sweep, then Rosaville.
\end{itemize}

The Final Key turns the next route into a combination of world cleanup
and major progression. Before approaching the monsters' headquarters,
collect every newly available medal and unique item, recover the Sphere
of Silence before its cutoff, obtain the Zenithian Armor, complete the
optional Cascade Cave, and retrieve the Staff of Transform from the
Royal Crypt.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Use the Final Key in Branca, Lakanaba, Haville, and Konenber.
\item
  Reach Rosaville, reveal Rosa's hidden tower, defeat Saroknight, and
  recover the Sphere of Silence.
\item
  Sail through the Shrine of Breaking Waves and claim the Zenithian
  Armor.
\item
  Use the Stone of Drought to clear optional Cascade Cave and obtain the
  Metal Babble Sword.
\item
  Enter the Royal Crypt and recover the Staff of Transform.
\item
  Sail through Riverton and activate the Colossus to cross the inland
  barrier.
\item
  Infiltrate Dire Palace in monster form, clear its treasures, and
  attend the conference.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Final Key Treasure Sweep}\label{final-key-treasure-sweep}

The Final Key opens every door encountered so far. Complete this
four-stop sweep before Rosaville so the party enters the next dungeons
with stronger equipment and three additional medals.

\subsection{Branca Castle}\label{branca-castle}

Enter the castle and go to the northeast jail. Open the Final Key door
and loot the three chests for \textbf{Mystic Acorns}, \textbf{120G}, and
\textbf{Small Medal 13}.

\subsection{Lakanaba Weapon Shop
Basement}\label{lakanaba-weapon-shop-basement}

Return to Lakanaba and descend into the basement of the weapon shop
where Taloon worked. Open the three locked chests for a \textbf{Strength
Seed}, \textbf{Ice Blade}, and \textbf{Chain Sickle}. The Ice Blade
provides +75 Attack and is a strong Hero or Ragnar weapon until the
Metal Babble or Zenithian Sword becomes available.

\subsection{Haville Jail}\label{haville-jail}

Enter the jail on Haville's east side and open the barred cells. Search
the pot in the first cell for \textbf{Small Medal 14}, then search the
pot in the third cell with the Yeti for \textbf{Small Medal 15}.

\subsection{Konenber Inn}\label{konenber-inn}

Go upstairs in the Konenber inn, open the Final Key door to the
northeast room, and take the \textbf{Sword of Decimation} from the
chest. It is cursed; store or sell it rather than equipping it.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 15/32.} The sweep adds one medal in Branca and
two in Haville. Lakanaba and Konenber provide equipment but no new
medals.

\end{achievementsetup}

\section{Rosaville and Rosa's Tower}\label{rosaville-and-rosas-tower}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 25\\
\textbf{Inn:} 9G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed boss reward:} Sphere of Silence\\
\textbf{Conservative cutoff:} Obtain the Sphere before the Dire Palace
conference

From the Small Medal King's island, sail southwest until finding a
river. Follow it south into the continent, choosing branches not blocked
by bridges, until the ship reaches the inland dead end. Disembark and
walk southeast to Rosaville.

The northern tower matches the shared dream in Izmit. Speak with the
residents to learn that Rosa is an elf whose ruby tears attracted human
cruelty and that Saro built the tower to protect her.

\subsection{Rosaville Shops}\label{rosaville-shops}

One shopkeeper near the entrance operates the item, weapon, armor, and
healing services.

Walk to the east side of Rosa's tower and stand where Saro appeared in
the Izmit dream. Use the \textbf{Flute of Uncovering} to reveal the
hidden staircase. The route to the top is linear and has no random
encounters. Unlock the upper door and confront Rosa's guard.

Use a physical-heavy party so spell sealing does not dismantle the
offense. Focus Saroknight rather than allowing summoned help to turn the
fight into an endless cycle; eliminate Chillanodons when their breath
damage becomes unsafe. The final EXP can exceed 6,800 when additional
enemies are defeated.

Saroknight drops the \textbf{Sphere of Silence}. Keep it in active
inventory or the vault.

\begin{guidenote}

\textbf{Critical Missable: Sphere of Silence}

The supplied NES sources identify this copy as permanently missable but
do not establish the exact event that removes Saroknight. Secure it
before the Dire Palace conference so the Esturk sequence cannot close
the opportunity.

\end{guidenote}

Speak with Rosa after the battle. She asks the Hero to stop Saro even
though doing so may mean killing him. Her Slime explains that the
\textbf{Staff of Transform} in the Royal Crypt can disguise the party
for entry into the monsters' palace. This conversation advances the
infiltration route, but it does not yet satisfy
\texttt{Rosa\textquotesingle{}s\ Fate}.

\section{Shrine of Breaking Waves}\label{shrine-of-breaking-waves}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 27\\
\textbf{Required key:} Final Key\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} 2 Small Medals, Zenithian Armor\\
\textbf{Travel rule:} Keep the party aboard the ship through the water
route

Sail to the cave directly north of the Small Medal King's island and
open its entrance with the Final Key. The shrine is short, but its
enemies are substantially stronger than the Rosaville region and many
resist one or more schools of magic.

\subsection{First Hidden Landing}\label{first-hidden-landing}

From the entrance, sail east and then north along the long waterway.
Seven rows of rock pillars divide the banks. Count upward from the
bottom. Immediately after passing the \textbf{fourth row}, disembark on
the left bank, walk west through the dark wall, and follow the hidden
passage south to the chest containing \textbf{Small Medal 16}.

Return to the ship and continue north. Follow the water around into the
large central rock maze. Sail south within the chamber, take the
northwest route through the rocks, turn west out of the maze, and
continue south to the small island with stairs.

Descend and open the two chests in the northwest part of the room. One
contains \textbf{Small Medal 17} and the other contains the
\textbf{Zenithian Armor}. Equip the armor on the Hero for +70 Defense
and its protective effects.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Underwater Oasis}

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the Shrine of Breaking Waves chest containing the
Zenithian Armor.

\end{achievementalert}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 17/32.} Both shrine medals are collected before
leaving.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{Shrine of Breaking Waves
Enemies}\label{shrine-of-breaking-waves-enemies}

Use Outside after opening the armor room and Return to a safe town. The
Hero now carries three of the four Zenithian equipment pieces; only the
Sword remains.

\section{After the Zenithian Armor}\label{after-the-zenithian-armor}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievement:} Underwater Oasis unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 17/32 found.
\item
  \textbf{Zenithian equipment:} Helm, Shield, and Armor obtained, 3/4.
\item
  \textbf{Missable item:} Sphere of Silence recovered from Saroknight
  before Esturk's Palace.
\item
  \textbf{Next objective:} Use the Stone of Drought at Cascade Cave,
  then retrieve the Staff of Transform from the Royal Crypt.
\end{itemize}

\section{Optional Side Route: Cascade
Cave}\label{optional-side-route-cascade-cave}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 28\\
\textbf{Required items:} Stone of Drought, Final Key\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Sandglass of Regression, Small Medal, Mask of
Corruption, 1,040G, Metal Babble Sword

Cascade Cave is optional for story progression but contains the
strongest immediately available physical weapon. Complete it now so
Ragnar can carry that weapon through the remaining required dungeons.

From Santeem, sail west until reaching the eastern coast of the
continent south of the Small Medal King's island. Follow the coast
south, land where forest meets the shoreline, and walk west to the cave
inside a small lake. The entrance is difficult to see from the ocean.

Walk north to the waterfall, stand directly in front of its center, and
use the \textbf{Stone of Drought}. The water recedes and exposes the
entrance. Open the Final Key door and take the \textbf{Sandglass of
Regression} from the first chest. It rewinds an ongoing battle and can
be used once per day. The supplied NES sources disagree on whether it
returns to the battle's beginning or only several rounds earlier.

\subsection{Cascade Cave Route}\label{cascade-cave-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Descend behind the Sandglass room.
\item
  Walk north, turn west beside the water, follow the path south and
  west, then continue north to the chest containing \textbf{Small Medal
  18}.
\item
  Walk south to the wall, turn west, and follow the linear route to the
  stairs.
\item
  On B3, walk directly south to the southwest chest containing the
  \textbf{Mask of Corruption}. It is cursed and must not be equipped
  despite its extreme Defense value.
\item
  Return to the stairs, go east, descend the stone steps, then continue
  east and south down the next steps to the chest containing
  \textbf{1,040G}.
\item
  Backtrack north, follow the path east and down, enter the narrow
  eastern passage, then descend the long southern steps and take the
  stairs at the end.
\item
  Follow the bottom floor's linear route to the \textbf{Metal Babble
  Sword}.
\end{enumerate}

Equip the Metal Babble Sword on Ragnar. Its +130 Attack gives him elite
sustained damage while allowing the Hero to continue using the current
sword until the Zenithian Sword is recovered.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 18/32.} Cascade Cave contains one medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{Cascade Cave Enemies}\label{cascade-cave-enemies}

Use Outside and Return after obtaining the sword. Rest and save in Endor
before the Royal Crypt.

\section{Royal Crypt and the Staff of
Transform}\label{royal-crypt-and-the-staff-of-transform}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 29-30\\
\textbf{Required key:} Final Key\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Staff of Antimagic, Small Medal, Staff of
Transform\\
\textbf{Recovery:} Unlimited HP/MP spring inside

From Endor, sail south down the river. Enter the shrine on the southern
cape where the river opens into the ocean. Open the northern Final Key
door and descend the stairs beyond it.

\subsection{Arrow-Tile Route}\label{arrow-tile-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  On the first lower floor, walk north and step onto either upward arrow
  column. Both deliver the party to the northern stairs.
\item
  On the next floor, walk east, take either arrow row, and descend the
  adjacent staircase.
\item
  Walk west and use the arrows that carry the party to the north end of
  B3. Open the nearby chest for the \textbf{Staff of Antimagic}.
\item
  From that chest, walk west and follow the passage south. Enter the
  eastern side corridor, follow it north without touching the arrows on
  the right, and enter the western room for \textbf{Small Medal 19}.
\item
  Return to the original B3 route and continue south along the lower
  wall. At the split, take the eastern passage to the staircase.
\item
  On the next floor, walk west and then north to the recovery spring.
  Stand beside it to restore the full party's HP and MP.
\item
  Go south from the spring, walk around the arrow tiles, and reach the
  staircase in the southwest corner.
\item
  Back on the separated portion of B1, walk west, follow the path south
  and east, then continue south past the nearby arrows to the final
  room.
\item
  Open the chest for the \textbf{Staff of Transform}.
\end{enumerate}

The Staff of Antimagic provides +50 Attack to Cristo, Brey, Nara, or
Mara and can suppress enemy spells when used in battle. The Staff of
Transform is a required field item; keep it in active inventory for Dire
Palace.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 19/32.} The Royal Crypt contains one medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

Metal Babbles are excellent for leveling, but they do not satisfy
\texttt{The\ One\ That\ Didn\textquotesingle{}t\ Get\ Away}, which
specifically requires a King Metal. Use the spring for bounded training
if desired, then leave with Outside or the exit arrows.

\section{Riverton}\label{riverton}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 31\\
\textbf{Inn:} 10G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed pickup:} Small Medal

Sail toward the mountain-ringed continent in the south-central world.
Enter its river from the north and take the eastern branch; the ship
transitions directly into Riverton even though the town is not visibly
marked on the world map.

Sail to the small island in the middle of town. Stand \textbf{two tiles
south of the person} there and search the southernmost grass tile for
\textbf{Small Medal 20}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 20/32.} Riverton contains one hidden ground
medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

A merchant on the west side wants a gas lighter than air so he can
finish a flying machine. This is a later objective; no item currently in
inventory satisfies it.

\subsection{Riverton Shops}\label{riverton-shops}

Rest and save, then sail out through Riverton's southern exit. Continue
to the end of the river, disembark, and walk south to the Shrine of the
Colossus.

\section{Shrine of the Colossus}\label{shrine-of-the-colossus}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 31\\
\textbf{Required key:} Final Key\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Small Medal, 640G, Agility Seed, Demon Hammer\\
\textbf{Trap chest:} Mimic\\
\textbf{Wagon restriction:} Choose the active four before crossing the
river

Open the Final Key door on the shrine's left side and approach the giant
statue. Enter through its lower body and walk north. Before taking the
central stairs, follow the outer path behind them to the chest
containing \textbf{Small Medal 21}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 21/32.} This is the Colossus's only medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

\subsection{Climbing the Colossus}\label{climbing-the-colossus}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Descend the central stairs. Walk west and enter the southern room for
  the chest containing \textbf{640G}.
\item
  Return to the central stairs, travel east, and take the northern
  staircase up.
\item
  On the next floor, walk northwest to the stairs along the north edge.
\item
  Walk west, then south through the opening. Turn east and continue to
  the two statues.
\item
  Enter the room south of the statues and take the \textbf{Agility
  Seed}.
\item
  Return north, go east from the statues, turn south through the
  opening, and follow the linear route to the next stairs.
\item
  Continue southeast and east, ignoring the two side rooms, and climb
  the next staircase.
\item
  Walk south out onto the statue's hand and open the chest for the
  \textbf{Demon Hammer}. It has +70 Attack but is cursed; store it
  rather than equipping it.
\item
  Return downstairs, walk north, and follow the passage into the central
  room. Take the staircase along its northern wall.
\item
  On the next floor, follow the path south, east, and north into the
  small stair room. The chest beyond contains a \textbf{Mimic}; fight it
  only for complete encounter coverage.
\item
  Climb to the head and jump through the \textbf{western opening} to
  land on the hand connected to the control route.
\item
  Follow the remaining stairs to the controls and activate the Colossus.
\end{enumerate}

The statue carries the party across the otherwise impassable river. The
wagon does not accompany the active group, so verify the four intended
Dire Palace members before activating the controls. A balanced group is
Hero / Ragnar / Alena / Cristo; the Staff of Transform works regardless
of who carries it.

\subsection{Colossus Enemies}\label{colossus-enemies}

After the Colossus stops, leave from either marked side exit and walk
southeast, then east to Dire Palace.

\section{Dire Palace Infiltration}\label{dire-palace-infiltration}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 31-32\\
\textbf{Required item:} Staff of Transform\\
\textbf{Fixed pickups:} 2 Small Medals, Aeolus' Shield, Lifeforce Nuts\\
\textbf{Trap chests:} 2 Mimics in the external treasure room

The palace admits the party while they appear to be monsters. Before
speaking to anyone, use the \textbf{Staff of Transform} until the party
takes a convincing monster form. If it produces humans or harmless
animals, use it again. The transformation expires after walking, so
reapply it whenever the disguise ends.

\subsection{Palace Treasure Route}\label{palace-treasure-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  From the entrance, walk north and descend the stairs on the left.
\item
  In the basement kitchen, search the pots for \textbf{Small Medal 22}.
\item
  Return to 1F and follow the eastern hallway. Use the Staff again
  before speaking with the monsters for optional intelligence.
\item
  Climb the southern stairs into the empty throne room.
\item
  Descend the northwest stairs, follow the hallway south, and leave
  through the exterior opening.
\item
  Walk west onto the grass and follow the narrow path north around the
  palace wall to the hidden staircase in the northwest corner.
\item
  Descend into the treasure room. The \textbf{two southern chests are
  Mimics}. The northern chests contain \textbf{Small Medal 23} and the
  \textbf{Aeolus' Shield}.
\item
  Exit and re-enter the palace to reset the route to the main entrance.
\end{enumerate}

Equip the Aeolus' Shield on Ragnar. Its +50 Defense is stronger than the
Dragon Shield, though the Dragon Shield remains useful when breath
protection is more important.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 23/32.} Dire Palace contains two medals, bringing
this section's total from 12 to 23.

\end{achievementsetup}

Before entering the conference room, follow the Minidemon upstairs, then
jump through the opening beside the meeting entrance. After landing
outside, cast StepGuard and cross east over the damaging floor. Descend
the stairs and search the ground directly in front of the tombstone for
hidden \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}. Re-enter the palace and restore the
monster disguise.

\subsection{Dire Palace Enemies}\label{dire-palace-enemies}

The disguise prevents forced battles when speaking with residents, but
random encounters and Mimic fights remain dangerous.

\section{The Monster Conference}\label{the-monster-conference}

Use the Staff of Transform immediately before entering the second-floor
conference room. Speak with every monster in the room. One directs the
disguised party to sit in the row behind the others. Take the indicated
seat and wait without leaving.

Saro arrives and announces that the miners beneath Aktemto have
uncovered the palace of Esturk, the Ruler of Evil, and that his
resurrection is imminent. The assembled monsters leave for Aktemto.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: A Monstrous Announcement}

\textbf{Trigger:} Remain disguised, take a seat in the Dire Palace
conference room, and witness Saro's announcement about Esturk.

\end{achievementalert}

Leave Dire Palace and cast Return to Haville. Rest, reorganize the
active party, and save, but do not enter Aktemto Mine yet; that begins
the next section.

\section{Dire Palace Route Completion
Check}\label{dire-palace-route-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Underwater Oasis and A Monstrous Announcement
  unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 23/32 found.
\item
  \textbf{Zenithian equipment:} Helm, Shield, and Armor obtained, 3/4.
\item
  \textbf{Unique equipment:} Metal Babble Sword assigned to Ragnar;
  Aeolus' Shield secured; cursed Mask of Corruption and Demon Hammer
  stored.
\item
  \textbf{Required items:} Sphere of Silence and Staff of Transform
  secured before advancing from Dire Palace to Aktemto.
\item
  \textbf{Optional route:} Cascade Cave fully cleared, including the
  Sandglass of Regression.
\item
  \textbf{Travel state:} Colossus activated; Dire Palace added to the
  Return list.
\item
  \textbf{Recovery:} Haville is the staging point for the return to
  Aktemto.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Aktemto Mine and Esturk's Palace.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Esturk's Palace and the
Balloon}\label{esturks-palace-and-the-balloon}

\section{Section Achievements: Esturk's Palace and the
Balloon}\label{section-achievements-esturks-palace-and-the-balloon}

\begin{guidetable}

There's Gas Inside & 10 & Acquire the hot air balloon & Defeat Esturk,

Saro's conference reveals that the miners beneath Aktemto have uncovered
Esturk's buried palace. This section follows that discovery to the Ruler
of Evil, recovers the Gas Canister after the battle, and converts it
into the balloon needed to reach the world's isolated regions. The
immediate return to Izmit then resolves the dream sequence begun before
Gardenbur.

The section's critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Return to Haville, prepare the active party, and enter Aktemto Mine.
\item
  Follow the newly opened mine route and collect its hidden Small Medal.
\item
  Use the recovery tile outside Esturk's Palace.
\item
  Clear both palace medals, 2,480G, the stat seeds, and the two Mimics.
\item
  Defeat the Rhinoking and Bengal guarding Esturk, then defeat Esturk
  himself.
\item
  Backtrack to the formerly guarded chest and recover the Gas Canister.
\item
  Deliver the canister in Riverton and receive the hot air balloon.
\item
  Return to Izmit and sleep at the inn to discover Rosa's fate.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Aktemto Revisited}\label{aktemto-revisited}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 32\\
\textbf{Inn:} 8G per person\\
\textbf{Required preparation:} Sphere of Silence already recovered from
Rosaville\\
\textbf{New mine pickup:} Small Medal

Cast Return to Haville after leaving Dire Palace, rest, save, and
organize the active party before walking northwest to Aktemto. The
earlier mine floors and their routes are unchanged. Descend past the
location where the Gunpowder Jar was found in Chapter 4; the new
excavation begins on B3 beside a corpse.

A safe Esturk party is \textbf{Hero / Alena / Ragnar / Cristo}. Alena
and Ragnar supply durable physical damage, Cristo offers Healus and
revival, and the Hero can alternate between offense and direct recovery.
\textbf{Hero / Alena / Ragnar / Mara} deals damage faster through
Blazemore but gives the Hero more healing responsibility. Use the safer
party unless the team is comfortably above the level target.

Verify the Sphere of Silence is already owned before entering the
expanded mine. It is not required to defeat Esturk, but it is a
documented missable whose precise cutoff is not identified consistently
by the supplied NES sources.

\subsection{Route Through the Expanded
Mine}\label{route-through-the-expanded-mine}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  From the B3 ladder beside the corpse, walk north to the water.
\item
  Turn west, then north, and follow the passage.
\item
  Take the first eastern branch.
\item
  In the next section, take the passage north.
\item
  When another path opens to the right, go east and descend the ladder
  to B4.
\item
  Walk northeast from the ladder and take the northern passage.
\item
  At the split, go east to the dead end beside the water.
\item
  Stand on the southern edge of the path against the water and use
  Search to uncover \textbf{Small Medal 24}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 24/32.} This is a hidden ground pickup rather
than a chest.

\end{achievementsetup}

Backtrack west and take the northern route. Pass through the opening
beside the wounded miner and continue north to the small building. Step
onto the white tile inside to restore the active party's HP and MP
completely. It does not remove poison or other persistent status
conditions, so cure those separately.

The palace entrance lies east of the recovery building. Use the tile
immediately before entering and return to it whenever the treasure sweep
consumes too many resources.

\subsection{Expanded Mine and Palace Approach
Enemies}\label{expanded-mine-and-palace-approach-enemies}

\section{Esturk's Palace Treasure
Route}\label{esturks-palace-treasure-route}

\textbf{Fixed treasure before Esturk:} 2 Small Medals, 2,480G, Strength
Seed, Agility Seed\\
\textbf{Trap chests:} 2 Mimics\\
\textbf{Post-boss treasure:} Gas Canister\\
\textbf{Escape restriction:} Outside fails until the party returns to
Aktemto Mine

Enter the palace and walk north through the first doorway.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Open the chest in the first small room for \textbf{Small Medal 25}.
\item
  The chest in the adjoining room to the left is a \textbf{Mimic}.
  Defeat it only after confirming the active party can recover from
  Defeat.
\item
  Continue west along the hall to the separate northwest room and take
  \textbf{2,480G}.
\item
  Continue south. Enter the room on the right and open its two chests
  for a \textbf{Strength Seed} and \textbf{Agility Seed}.
\item
  Take the southern stairs up.
\item
  Follow the path north and east. Ignore the first staircase and
  continue south to the stairs leading down.
\item
  Follow the lower route to the room with two chests. One is another
  \textbf{Mimic} and the other contains \textbf{Small Medal 26}. Open
  both only when the party is ready for the trap encounter.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 26/32.} Esturk's Palace contains two medals,
bringing the section total from 23/32 to 26/32.

\end{achievementsetup}

A skeleton blocks the central treasure chest beyond this room. It cannot
be removed directly. Leave that chest for the return trip after Esturk.

Walk north from the treasure room and enter the opening northwest of the
skeleton. Follow the narrow western passage to the staircase. The
remaining ascent is linear and ends in Esturk's chamber.

\section{Esturk's Antechamber}\label{esturks-antechamber}

Two monsters stand between the party and Esturk. Refuse their demand to
leave and defeat one Rhinoking and one Bengal. Together they award
\textbf{970 EXP and 212G}, before any possible item drop. Use ordinary
focused attacks, preserve MP, then restore the party fully before
approaching Esturk.

Esturk may begin asleep, but this is not a harmless opening. His eerie
light can damage the entire party before he wakes. Use the early turns
for direct damage rather than building a large stack of buffs that his
freezing wave can erase.

Alena's two attacks and critical-hit rate make her the best burst
attacker. Ragnar supplies dependable damage with the Metal Babble Sword.
If Mara is active, Blazemore is useful, but her AI may choose Firevolt
even though it is ineffective; Normal tactics are safer than relying on
a specific spell. Cristo provides the more stable route through Healus
and revival.

When Esturk takes a deep breath, assume the following action can defeat
or critically wound a character. Use Healall preemptively if the party
is already damaged. After Esturk wakes, his two actions make low-HP
casters especially vulnerable, so the Hero should abandon offense
whenever one bad round could cause a collapse.

Esturk's 900 HP rewards aggressive but controlled damage. Do not
repeatedly replace buffs erased by the freezing wave; restore HP and
keep attacking. Victory awards \textbf{15,000 EXP}.

\section{Saro's Arrival and the Gas
Canister}\label{saros-arrival-and-the-gas-canister}

After Esturk falls, Saro arrives and comments that Esturk's evolution
was incomplete. Before he can confront the party, another monster
reports that humans have abducted Rosa. Saro leaves immediately to find
her.

Backtrack through the palace. The skeleton guarding the central chest is
gone. Open the chest for the \textbf{Gas Canister}. This is the only
required post-boss pickup; do not leave the palace without it.

Outside cannot be cast inside Esturk's Palace. Continue back to the
cavern, use the recovery tile if needed, and return to the original
Aktemto Mine floors. Outside works once the party is back in the mine.
Cast it there, then Return to Riverton.

\section{Riverton and the Hot Air
Balloon}\label{riverton-and-the-hot-air-balloon}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 32-33\\
\textbf{Required item:} Gas Canister\\
\textbf{Reward:} Hot air balloon

Visit the merchant on Riverton's northwest side who previously lacked a
suitable lifting gas. Give him the \textbf{Gas Canister}. If he does not
hand over the finished craft immediately, stay at the inn and speak with
him again the following morning. He gives the party the \textbf{hot air
balloon}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: There's Gas Inside}

\textbf{Trigger:} Receive the completed hot air balloon from Riverton's
inventor.

\end{achievementalert}

Leave Riverton and step onto the balloon, then press the interaction
button to board. The balloon crosses mountains, shoals, and other
terrain that blocks the ship. Casting Return brings the party's major
vehicles to the destination region, so neither the ship nor balloon is
permanently stranded.

One supplied route mentions the old-man island medal here. That is the
same right-pot medal already counted as \textbf{Small Medal 6} during
the Magic Key sweep. If it was missed, collect it now at the small
island shack north of Haville, but do not increment the route total
twice.

\section{Izmit's Second Dream}\label{izmits-second-dream}

Before flying to the desert marked on the Treasure Map, return to Izmit
and stay at the inn. The second dream shows humans abusing the captive
Rosa. Saro arrives too late to save her. As Rosa lies dying, she asks
him to abandon his revenge against humanity; he refuses and vows that
mankind will pay.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Rosa's Fate}

\textbf{Trigger:} Sleep at the Izmit inn after Esturk's defeat and
acquisition of the balloon, then watch the second Rosa and Saro dream
through its conclusion.

\end{achievementalert}

This scene explains why Saro abandoned Esturk's chamber and why his
campaign against humanity intensifies. It completes the Rosa dream
sequence but does not yet bring the party into direct conflict with
Saro.

\section{Balloon Route Completion
Check}\label{balloon-route-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} There's Gas Inside and Rosa's Fate unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 26/32 found; the old-man island medal is
  already included as medal 6.
\item
  \textbf{Major victory:} Esturk defeated for 15,000 EXP.
\item
  \textbf{Travel:} Hot air balloon acquired; ship and wagon remain
  available.
\item
  \textbf{Key items:} Gas Canister delivered; Sphere of Silence and
  Staff of Transform retained or vaulted.
\item
  \textbf{Treasure:} All three new medals, 2,480G, both stat seeds, and
  Gas Canister collected; both Mimics resolved.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} The forest at the Treasure Map's desert
  marker, containing Elfville and the World Tree.
\end{itemize}

The balloon opens the two regions that complete the ascent to Zenithia.
The first is the desert settlement of \textbf{Elfville}, where the World
Tree holds both an injured Zenithian and the final piece of the Hero's
equipment. The second is the isolated central island containing
Gottside, the Shrine of the Horn, and Zenithian Tower.

The critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Fly to the desert marker shown on the Treasure Map and enter Elfville.
\item
  Reduce the active party to three members before entering the World
  Tree.
\item
  Recover the Staff of Healing, pluck a Leaf of the World Tree, and
  collect the Dew of the World Tree.
\item
  Rescue Lucia and open the nearby chest for the Zenithian Sword.
\item
  Fly to the isolated central island and collect Small Medal 27 in
  Gottside.
\item
  Clear the Shrine of the Horn for the Baron's Horn and hidden Dress of
  Radiance.
\item
  Equip the Hero with all four pieces of Zenithian equipment and enter
  Zenithian Tower.
\item
  Collect all five tower treasures, including Small Medal 28, and ride
  the cloud to Zenithia.
\item
  Enter Zenithian Castle with Lucia active, collect Small Medal 29, and
  recruit Doran.
\item
  Speak with the Master Dragon and receive the Zenithian Sword's
  blessing.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Elfville and the World Tree}\label{elfville-and-the-world-tree}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 33\\
\textbf{Inn:} 20G\\
\textbf{Required active party size:} Exactly 3 before entering the
tree\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Staff of Healing, Dew of the World Tree,
Zenithian Sword\\
\textbf{Renewable pickup:} Leaf of the World Tree

Board the balloon outside Riverton or Izmit and check the Treasure Map.
Fly to the large desert on the eastern side of the world, then land
beside the small cluster of trees at the map's marked location. Enter
the trees to reach Elfville and the World Tree.

Speak with the elves before climbing. They report that a voice from the
tree is asking rescuers to come in a group of three. Leave town, use the
wagon to reduce the active party to \textbf{exactly three characters},
then re-enter. Lucia will become the fourth member after her rescue, so
a full party cannot accept her.

A safe climbing team is \textbf{Hero / Ragnar or Alena / Cristo or
Nara}. The Hero supplies flexible offense and Healall, a physical
attacker limits dependence on MP, and a dedicated healer stabilizes the
three-person party. The World Tree has no conventional exit problem:
stepping off a branch drops the party safely outside Elfville, but an
accidental fall forces a complete reclimb.

\begin{landscape}

Both pieces are worthwhile but optional. Preserve enough gold for
healing and supplies if buying either one; no purchase is required to
complete the World Tree.

\subsection{World Tree Treasure Route}\label{world-tree-treasure-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Enter the trunk and walk north into the small upper room.
\item
  Climb the ladder there and open the chest for the \textbf{Staff of
  Healing}. It has 5 attack power, can be equipped by Cristo, Brey, or
  Nara, and casts Healmore when used in battle.
\item
  While standing on the leaf-covered portions of the upper floors, use
  Search to pluck a \textbf{Leaf of the World Tree}. The party can carry
  only one at a time, and the leaf fully revives one fallen character.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Arboreal Vandal}

\textbf{Trigger:} Search the leaf-covered floor inside the World Tree
and receive a Leaf of the World Tree.

\end{achievementalert}

If the party already possesses a Leaf of the World Tree, the tree will
not supply another. Use the existing leaf when revival is needed, then
search the leaves again so the plucking event is recorded.

Return down the first ladder to the entrance floor and begin the main
ascent:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Walk south from the entrance, turn east into the narrow passage, and
  follow it to the next ladder.
\item
  On the next floor, go east from the ladder and then south onto the
  branch.
\item
  Cross the leaves west along the southern edge and enter the small room
  to the north.
\item
  Climb the ladder inside that room.
\item
  On the next floor, walk south onto the leaves and climb the nearby
  ladder.
\item
  Open the chest at the top for the \textbf{Dew of the World Tree},
  which restores the active party's HP when used in battle.
\item
  Descend to the previous floor and return north to the ladder used to
  reach it.
\item
  Continue north onto the northeastern branch, then follow the leaves
  south and clockwise around the trunk.
\item
  Enter the small room in the northwest corner and climb its ladder.
\item
  Walk north onto the leaves and circle counterclockwise until standing
  southeast of the ladder.
\item
  Find the next ladder directly east of the trunk and climb to the top
  of the World Tree.
\end{enumerate}

Move carefully on every exterior branch. Walking off an unguarded edge
returns the party to the ground outside Elfville.

\section{Lucia and the Zenithian
Sword}\label{lucia-and-the-zenithian-sword}

From the final ladder, walk a few steps west and follow the southwestern
branch toward the center. Speak with \textbf{Lucia}, the injured
Zenithian whose voice was heard from below. Her wings were damaged by
monsters, so agree to escort her home. She joins the three-character
active party as a temporary fourth member.

Lucia identifies the final piece of Zenithian equipment. From her
position, walk southeast onto the leaves and open the chest for the
\textbf{Zenithian Sword}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: The Highest Branches}

\textbf{Trigger:} Open the chest near Lucia at the top of the World Tree
and obtain the Zenithian Sword.

\end{achievementalert}

The sword initially has \textbf{65 attack power}, can be equipped only
by the Hero, and produces the Disperse effect when used as an item. Its
current attack value is intentionally modest; the Master Dragon can
restore its full strength. With the Sword, Helm, Armor, and Shield now
secured, the Zenithian set is complete at \textbf{4/4}.

The fastest exit is to step from the top branch and fall outside
Elfville. Rebuild a four-character active party at the wagon, but keep
Lucia available: she must be brought into Zenithian Castle to open the
route to Doran.

\section{The Central Island and
Gottside}\label{the-central-island-and-gottside}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 34-35\\
\textbf{Inn:} 12G per person\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Small Medal\\
\textbf{Major destination:} Shrine of the Horn, then Zenithian Tower

Board the balloon and fly to the small island near the center of the
world. It lies due south of Loch Tower and is completely enclosed by
terrain the ship cannot cross. Land on the island; its interior expands
into a larger region, and further balloon travel within it is not
possible.

From the landing area, travel northeast around the mountain range,
continue east, and then turn south to reach Gottside. Speak with the
residents to learn about the tower southwest of town and the wounded
Zenithian urging the Hero to meet the Master Dragon.

In the far northeast corner of town, descend the stairs into the small
underground room. Search the \textbf{eastern dresser} for \textbf{Small
Medal 27}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 27/32.} This dresser is Gottside's only routed
medal.

\end{achievementsetup}

satisfy the tower entrance. The required shield is specifically the
\textbf{Zenithian Shield} already recovered from Gardenbur.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: The One That Didn't Get Away (deferred)}

King Metals appear in the hills northeast of Gottside, but the dedicated
hunt belongs in Side Activities and Final Checks. A natural victory
still unlocks the achievement; no grinding is required here.

\end{achievementsetup}

\section{Shrine of the Horn}\label{shrine-of-the-horn}

\textbf{Encounters:} None\\
\textbf{Required treasure:} Baron's Horn\\
\textbf{Hidden treasure:} Dress of Radiance

Rest and save in Gottside, then travel far east. Enter the mountain ring
from its open eastern side and continue to the shrine.

The visible chest and the hidden chest require different falls:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Walk north from the entrance and drop through the \textbf{large hole}.
\item
  On the covered platform below, take the southeastern stairs.
\item
  Open the chest for the \textbf{Baron's Horn}. Retain it; it can call
  the wagon when the party is separated from it later.
\item
  Return up the stairs and walk north across the covered platform.
\item
  Drop through the \textbf{smaller hole} onto the elevator at the bottom
  of the shaft.
\item
  Step on the switch to raise the elevator to the top.
\item
  Step off and use the stairs in the northwest corner.
\item
  Follow the linear passage back to the floor that held the elevator.
\item
  With the elevator moved, descend the staircase that had been concealed
  beneath it.
\item
  Open the northern basement chest for the \textbf{Dress of Radiance}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Hidden Treasure - Shrine of the Horn}

\textbf{Trigger:} Move the elevator, descend the staircase it concealed,
and open the basement chest containing the Dress of Radiance.

\end{achievementalert}

The Dress of Radiance has \textbf{45 defense} and can be equipped by the
Hero, Alena, Mara, or Nara. It can reflect hostile magic, but it can
also reflect friendly spells and prevent healing from reaching its
wearer. Keep that drawback in mind before using it in the final
dungeons.

Return to Gottside, heal, save, and equip the Hero with the complete
Zenithian set.

\section{Zenithian Tower Entry Check}\label{zenithian-tower-entry-check}

The tower rejects the party unless the Hero is actively wearing all four
pieces:

Equip all four pieces before touching the tower entrance. Possessing an
item in the bag or placing it on another character is not enough. Once
the party is inside, equipment can be changed, though the set is already
well suited to the climb.

\section{Zenithian Tower}\label{zenithian-tower}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 35\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Magic Potion, Mystic Acorns, Small Medal,
Mysterious Bolero, Dragon Shield\\
\textbf{Exit:} Orange cloud at the summit

The tower contains several optional branches that end in treasure. Clear
each branch when first reached to avoid retracing most of the climb.

\subsection{Treasure and Summit Route}\label{treasure-and-summit-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  From the entrance, walk north through the first intersection and turn
  west near the top of the floor.
\item
  Follow the western path to the northwest staircase.
\item
  Climb it, walk northeast, and take the next stairs to the exterior
  chest containing a \textbf{Magic Potion}.
\item
  Retrace the entire branch to the first floor.
\item
  This time travel east to the staircase in the northeast corner.
\item
  Follow the upper route south, enter the interior doorway, and circle
  to the chest containing \textbf{Mystic Acorns}.
\item
  Return outside, continue west, climb two ladders, and enter the
  doorway at the top.
\item
  Take the stairs on the left in the southwest corner. On the next
  floor, reach the southwest stairs and climb to the dead-end room
  containing \textbf{Small Medal 28}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 28/32.} Return to the three-stair room after
collecting it; the medal branch does not continue upward.

\end{achievementsetup}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{8}
\tightlist
\item
  Back in the first room entered from outside, take the southeastern
  stairs and follow the linear route until it returns outdoors.
\item
  Walk west, climb the ladder, and continue west to the doorway with
  stairs below it.
\item
  Descend those stairs and follow the winding interior path to a doorway
  beside another staircase.
\item
  Take the exterior doorway first. Follow the branch to its dead end and
  open the chest for the \textbf{Mysterious Bolero}.
\item
  Backtrack to the previous fork and take the staircase that was
  skipped.
\item
  Outside again, walk north into the doorway, climb the southeastern
  stairs, and use the ladder to enter the large upper room.
\item
  From that doorway, go east and follow the path to the \textbf{Dragon
  Shield} chest.
\item
  Return to the doorway, travel northwest, and then go south into the
  carpeted passage.
\item
  Follow the carpet east, turn south to the exterior path, and continue
  along the linear ascent.
\item
  Climb the final stairs and ladder, then stand on the summit platform.
  An orange cloud carries the party to Zenithia.
\end{enumerate}

The \textbf{Mysterious Bolero} has 37 defense, can be worn by Cristo,
Nara, Mara, or Brey, and can absorb the MP cost of a spell directed at
its wearer. The free \textbf{Dragon Shield} has 30 defense and reduces
fire and blizzard damage for the Hero or Ragnar.

\section{Zenithian Castle}\label{zenithian-castle}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 35-36\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Small Medal\\
\textbf{Renewable gift:} Dew of the World Tree\\
\textbf{Companion:} Doran\\
\textbf{Primary objective:} Master Dragon

Place Lucia in the active party before entering the castle. She leaves
upon arriving home, completing her escort and making Doran available.
The wagon also reaches Zenithia, so restore the preferred four-character
formation after Lucia departs.

From the entrance, do not climb the large central stairs yet. Walk
northwest, unlock the door, and enter the room directly north. The
resident monster gives the party a \textbf{Dew of the World Tree}
whenever none is currently owned. The Dew restores the active party's HP
in battle and is limited to one at a time.

Enter the room immediately to the right and search the three dressers
for \textbf{Small Medal 29}.

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 29/32.} This is the final medal in the current
section.

\end{achievementsetup}

On the lower castle level near the House of Healing, find Lucia again.
Speak with her and accept the help of \textbf{Doran}, a young Kordra who
joins as an AI-controlled companion for the remaining journey.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Zenithia's Finest}

\textbf{Trigger:} Escort Lucia home, speak with her in Zenithia, and
allow Doran to join the party.

\end{achievementalert}

Speak with the castle's residents before approaching the throne. Their
accounts connect the Hero's parents, Esturk's incomplete evolution, the
Golden Bracelet, and Necrosaro's retreat beneath the world. They also
explain the function of the Baron's Horn and the route through the
clouds.

Climb to the Master Dragon and speak with him. He directs the Hero to
stop Necrosaro, grants the Hero \textbf{5,000 EXP}, and restores the
Zenithian Sword from 65 to \textbf{110 attack power}. The blessing may
leave the sword unequipped, so check the Hero's equipment afterward and
equip it again if desired. The Metal Babble Sword remains stronger in
raw attack, but the restored Zenithian Sword is the Hero's intended
weapon and retains its Disperse item effect.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Humans are Curious Creatures}

\textbf{Trigger:} Reach the top of Zenithian Castle and complete the
conversation with the Master Dragon.

\end{achievementalert}

The southwestern cloud passage descends to the dungeon leading beneath
the world. Do not enter it yet; that begins the next section.

\section{Zenithia Route Completion
Check}\label{zenithia-route-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} Arboreal Vandal, The Highest Branches, Hidden
  Treasure - Shrine of the Horn, Zenithia's Finest, and Humans are
  Curious Creatures unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Achievement points:} 25 points earned in this section.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 29/32 found, advancing from 26/32 through
  Gottside, Zenithian Tower, and Zenithian Castle.
\item
  \textbf{Zenithian equipment:} Sword, Helm, Armor, and Shield obtained,
  4/4; Sword blessed to 110 attack power.
\item
  \textbf{Companions:} Lucia returned safely to Zenithia; Doran joined
  the party.
\item
  \textbf{Key items:} Baron's Horn secured for the separated-wagon route
  ahead.
\item
  \textbf{Major treasure:} Staff of Healing, renewable Leaf and Dew of
  the World Tree, Dress of Radiance, Mysterious Bolero, and Dragon
  Shield obtained.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} The cloud passage from Zenithia and the
  Final Cave.
\end{itemize}

\chapter{The Dark World and the Four
Guardians}\label{the-dark-world-and-the-four-guardians}

\section{Section Achievement: The Dark World and the Four
Guardians}\label{section-achievement-the-dark-world-and-the-four-guardians}

\begin{guidetable}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2580}}
  >{\raggedleft\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.0739}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2915}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.3766}}@{}}

\color{GuideGreen}\bfseries Achievement
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedleft

And Then There Were None & 10 & Defeat the four guardians of the Dark
World & Defeat Radimvice, Anderoug, Gigademon, and Infurnus Shadow in
their four barrier shrines. \\

}

\end{guidetable}

Necrosaro has withdrawn beneath the world and sealed his palace behind a
four-part barrier. Reaching that barrier first requires a long descent
through the \textbf{Final Cave}, the dungeon connecting Zenithia to the
Dark World. The party then uses the Last Refuge as a base while
defeating one guardian in each corner of the Dark World.

The critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Use the southwestern cloud passage in Zenithia and enter the mountain
  cave below.
\item
  Complete the Final Cave treasure sweep, including Small Medal 30.
\item
  Exit into the Dark World and activate the Last Refuge as the recovery
  point.
\item
  Defeat Radimvice in the northeast shrine.
\item
  Defeat the three Anderougs in the southeast shrine.
\item
  Search outside the southwest shrine for Small Medal 31, solve its
  statue room, and defeat Gigademon.
\item
  Defeat Infurnus Shadow in the northwest shrine.
\item
  Return to the Last Refuge after Necrosaro's barrier falls, heal, and
  save.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Final Cave}\label{final-cave}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 36-38\\
\textbf{Wagon access:} Available throughout the dungeon\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Dress of Radiance, Small Medal, 3 Lifeforce
Nuts, Water Flying Clothes, Staff of Jubilation, Demon Armor, 3,280G,
Agility Seed, Strength Seed, Mirror Shield\\
\textbf{Trap chest:} 1 Mimic

In Zenithian Castle, return to the lower room whose southwestern doorway
opens onto the clouds. Walk outside and drop through the hole. The party
lands beside the cave enclosed by the mountains near Gottside.

Bring the \textbf{Baron's Horn} even though it is not needed during this
section; it will be required after crossing Necrosaro's Palace in the
next section. The wagon accompanies the party through the Final Cave, so
rotate reserve healers into use and draw on their MP instead of
exhausting only the active party. Carry the Staff of Antimagic or Sphere
of Silence for Infurnus Shadow and enough Full Moon Herbs to address
paralysis.

justify. Running is reasonable when Master Malice, Ogrebasher, or a
difficult mixed group would consume the resources needed to finish the
treasure route.

\subsection{Entrance, Medal, and Poisoned Treasure
Room}\label{entrance-medal-and-poisoned-treasure-room}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Walk north into the second room and open the door along its northern
  wall.
\item
  Open the chest inside for a second \textbf{Dress of Radiance}. Its
  spell reflection can also reject friendly healing, so do not equip it
  automatically.
\item
  Return south, take the western opening, and follow the long spiraling
  hallway.
\item
  At the first western side passage, enter the arrow-tile area.
\item
  Step onto the rightmost column of arrows pointing south. The tiles
  carry the party into the enclosed area.
\item
  Walk directly south and open the chest for \textbf{Small Medal 30}.
\item
  Return north and use the left column of arrows at the top to leave the
  enclosure.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 30/32.} The achievement must remain locked; two
routed medals are still outstanding.

\end{achievementsetup}

Return to the spiraling hallway and continue along it. Pass the first
western branch for the moment and keep following the spiral toward its
central end. Take the later western passage into the large room covered
with damaging floor.

Cast StepGuard, then cross to the far northwest corner and open the
isolated chest for \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}. Move to the southwest corner
and follow the safe southern edge east. A short row of arrow tiles
deposits the party beside the chest containing \textbf{Water Flying
Clothes}.

Return to the spiraling hallway and backtrack to the earlier western
branch that was skipped.

\subsection{Staff, Demon Armor, and Four-Chest
Cache}\label{staff-demon-armor-and-four-chest-cache}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Follow the earlier western branch into the next room.
\item
  Enter the small side room to the west and open its chest for the
  \textbf{Staff of Jubilation}.
\item
  Return to the main path, travel south, and enter the southeastern
  passage.
\item
  Cast StepGuard before crossing the damaging floor. The isolated chest
  in the southeast corner is a \textbf{Mimic}; fight it only if its
  experience and possible Dancer's Costume are worth the risk.
\item
  Continue west and then north along the linear path to the northwest
  stairs.
\item
  On the next floor, walk west and then south around the pit.
\item
  Use the left column of southbound arrow tiles to reach the chest
  beside the water. It contains the cursed \textbf{Demon Armor}.
\item
  From the chest, walk east and step north onto the eastbound arrows
  just south of the small pond.
\item
  Descend the nearby stairs to the room with four grouped chests.
\item
  Open all four for \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}, \textbf{3,280G}, an
  \textbf{Agility Seed}, and a \textbf{Strength Seed}.
\item
  Take the staircase in the southwest corner.
\end{enumerate}

The Demon Armor provides 60 defense but is cursed. Store it rather than
equipping it during this route. The Water Flying Clothes remain useful
for Brey, Mara, or Nara, while the Staff of Jubilation can be carried by
a caster as a backup weapon.

\subsection{Boat and Lower Tower}\label{boat-and-lower-tower}

Walk north to the boat and sail directly east to the small island
containing the next staircase. Follow the linear corridor to another
descent. At the large door, continue north through the long hallway
until reaching a pit.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Deliberately fall through the pit to land beside the chest containing
  the \textbf{Mirror Shield}.
\item
  Drop through the central opening one more floor.
\item
  Use the nearby stairs to climb back up one level.
\item
  Open the chest along the eastern wall for the third set of
  \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts}.
\item
  Descend again by the stairs or the central opening.
\item
  Take the northern staircase down.
\item
  Walk south and leave the tower to enter the Dark World.
\end{enumerate}

The Mirror Shield has 45 defense and can be equipped by the Hero,
Ragnar, or Cristo. It can reflect hostile magic, so compare that effect
with the elemental resistance and higher defense of the wielder's
current shield.

\section{The Last Refuge}\label{the-last-refuge}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 38-40\\
\textbf{Services:} Full HP/MP recovery and saving\\
\textbf{Return destination:} Available after it is visited\\
\textbf{Objective:} Defeat one guardian in each corner of the Dark World

Enter the shrine immediately beside the dungeon exit. Walk north and
approach the flames. A Zenithian appears, restores the party's HP and
MP, offers to record the adventure, and explains the barrier protecting
Necrosaro's Palace.

Use the Last Refuge after every guardian. Return works between the
refuge and the surface world, but it does not work from inside a
guardian's den; step outside first. Because recovery is free and nearby,
spend MP aggressively during each boss battle rather than saving it for
all four.

A dependable active party is \textbf{Hero / Alena / Ragnar / Cristo},
with Doran accompanying them. The Hero covers emergency Healall or
Healusall, Alena and Ragnar provide sustained physical damage, and
Cristo supplies group healing and revival. Brey is useful when a
guardian is vulnerable to magic or when Bikill is desired, but Cristo is
the safer default.

The four dens can be cleared in any order. This route proceeds clockwise
from the northeast so that the Gigademon medal is collected before the
final guardian.

\begin{landscape}

\section{Northeast Guardian:
Radimvice}\label{northeast-guardian-radimvice}

\textbf{Location:} Northeast corner of the Dark World\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Zombie Mail\\
\textbf{Encounter:} Radimvice with 3 Demighouls

Enter the Den of Radimvice and take the first stairs. On the next floor,
use the northeastern staircase first. Open the chest directly south for
the cursed \textbf{Zombie Mail}, then return to the previous floor and
take the southwestern staircase to Radimvice's chamber.

\chaptermark{The Dark World and the Four Guardians}

Radimvice begins protected by Bounce and can use Blizzard and Infermost
while acting twice. The three Demighouls are difficult to hit, can
divide, and make the encounter unstable if left alive. Set tactics to
Normal and remove the Demighouls first. Cristo's Defeat can work against
them, while Lightning provides a more direct option if reflected damage
from Radimvice's Bounce can be avoided.

Once the adds are gone, use Alena and Ragnar for physical damage. The
Hero should alternate between attacking and healing. Do not spend
repeated turns stripping Bounce when Radimvice can restore it; physical
pressure is safer. With the initial three Demighouls and no divisions,
the encounter awards \textbf{7,974 EXP and 189G}.

Leave the den, cast Return to the Last Refuge, recover, and save.

\begin{landscape}

\section{Southeast Guardian:
Anderoug}\label{southeast-guardian-anderoug}

\textbf{Location:} Southeast corner of the Dark World\\
\textbf{Encounter:} 3 Anderougs

The Den of Anderoug has a short, direct route to the throne room. Either
side path reaches the guardian encounter.

\chaptermark{The Dark World and the Four Guardians}

Every Anderoug can call another Anderoug, so killing them one at a time
can extend the battle. Use Lightning, Explodet, Infermost, or Blizzard
to damage the entire formation. Defeat can also remove them outright.
Alena's critical hits and Ragnar's attacks should finish survivors in
the same round whenever possible.

The initial trio is worth \textbf{3,090 EXP and 900G} before any
reinforcements or possible drops. Return to the Last Refuge afterward
and restore the party.

\section{Southwest Guardian:
Gigademon}\label{southwest-guardian-gigademon}

\textbf{Location:} Southwest corner of the Dark World\\
\textbf{Hidden treasure:} Small Medal\\
\textbf{Encounter:} Gigademon

Approach the southwest shrine through the narrow grass path between the
trees; there is no need to cross the surrounding swamp. Before solving
the interior route, collect the section's second medal:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Enter the shrine area and take the exterior path lined with
  tombstones.
\item
  Follow it clockwise around the building, using StepGuard over the
  damaging ground.
\item
  At the far end in the northwest corner, use Search to uncover
  \textbf{Small Medal 31}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{achievementsetup}

\textbf{Achievement Setup: Small Medal Collector (deferred)}

\textbf{Running total: 31/32.} Do not claim the achievement yet; the
final routed medal is inside Necrosaro's Palace.

\end{achievementsetup}

Exit and re-enter the shrine to reset the approach. Walk west and then
south to the first staircase, descend it, immediately climb back up, and
take the staircase farther south.

The next room contains five statues that mirror the party's movement.
Contact with a statue ejects the party but causes no other penalty. From
the room entrance, move all the way west, walk north enough to pull the
statues behind the obstacles, then move east and continue north through
the opening. Cast StepGuard before entering Gigademon's chamber.

Gigademon asks the Hero to inspect the ground behind the party. Turning
around or speaking to him again begins the fight.

Gigademon acts twice and can use Defense against the party. His large HP
pool makes this an endurance fight, but his offense is primarily
physical. Normal tactics with direct attacks are sufficient; Surround
can reduce the danger while Alena and Ragnar work through his HP. Keep
the Hero ready to heal after critical hits rather than waiting for the
AI to react.

Return to the Last Refuge, heal, and save.

\section{Northwest Guardian: Infurnus
Shadow}\label{northwest-guardian-infurnus-shadow}

\textbf{Location:} Northwest corner of the Dark World\\
\textbf{Encounter:} Infurnus Shadow\\
\textbf{Recommended tool:} Staff of Antimagic or Sphere of Silence

The final routed den places its guardian immediately inside. Heal before
entering because there is no treasure branch or preliminary encounter.

\end{guidetable}

Infurnus Shadow can act twice and combines Explodet with strong blaze
and blizzard attacks. Use the \textbf{Staff of Antimagic} or
\textbf{Sphere of Silence} to seal Explodet. Elemental breath remains
dangerous after the spell is blocked, so keep the party above the point
where two group attacks could cause a collapse.

Use Normal tactics while recovery is stable and switch to Defensive if
the Hero and Cristo cannot keep pace. Alena and Ragnar should maintain
physical pressure; the Hero uses Healusall when several characters are
damaged. The possible Sword of Miracles is valuable, but the guardian's
defeat is the required outcome.

When Infurnus Shadow falls, all four guardians have been defeated and
the barrier around Necrosaro's Palace disappears.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: And Then There Were None}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Radimvice, Anderoug, Gigademon, and Infurnus
Shadow so that all four sections of the barrier surrounding Necrosaro's
Palace vanish.

\end{achievementalert}

Return to the Last Refuge one final time. Restore HP and MP, save,
confirm the \textbf{Baron's Horn} is in the party inventory, and stop
before entering Necrosaro's Palace.

\section{Dark World Route Completion
Check}\label{dark-world-route-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievement:} And Then There Were None unlocked for 10 points.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 31/32 found, advancing from 29/32 through the
  Final Cave and Gigademon's shrine.
\item
  \textbf{Guardian status:} Radimvice, Anderoug, Gigademon, and Infurnus
  Shadow defeated; Necrosaro's barrier removed.
\item
  \textbf{Recovery:} Last Refuge available through Return for free HP/MP
  restoration and saving.
\item
  \textbf{Key item:} Baron's Horn retained for calling the wagon after
  Necrosaro's Palace.
\item
  \textbf{Major treasure:} All ten fixed cave rewards collected; Mimic
  defeated or deliberately skipped; Zombie Mail recovered from
  Radimvice's den.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Necrosaro's Palace, containing Small Medal
  32, the Sage's Stone, and the route to Necrosaro's mountain.
\end{itemize}

\hyperref[table-of-contents]{Return to Table of Contents}

\chapter{Necrosaro's Palace and the
Ending}\label{necrosaros-palace-and-the-ending}

\section{Section Achievements: Necrosaro's Palace and the
Ending}\label{section-achievements-necrosaros-palace-and-the-ending}

\begin{guidetable}

Hidden Treasure - Necrosaro's Palace & 5 & Find the hidden treasure in

Is This The End? & 25 & Defeat Necrosaro & Defeat all seven forms in one

We Don't Need Another Hero & 25 & Defeat Necrosaro without the hero in

\end{guidetable}

With the four guardians defeated, the barrier around Necrosaro's Palace
is gone. This section completes the palace treasure route, secures the
Sage's Stone, opens the path to the mountain, and covers all seven forms
of the final battle. Because defeating Necrosaro leads directly into the
ending rather than a postgame save, preserve a repeatable in-game save
before the fight for the two party-composition achievements and any
unfinished side activities.

The critical path is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Enter Necrosaro's Palace and avoid the roaming Clay Dolls.
\item
  Collect Small Medal 32 from the poisoned basement.
\item
  Find the concealed passage between the statues and recover the Sage's
  Stone.
\item
  Collect the Lifeforce Nuts and Staff of Thunder.
\item
  Manipulate both elevators and leave through the palace's rear exit.
\item
  Use the Baron's Horn outside to summon the wagon.
\item
  Return to the Last Refuge, heal, save, and prepare the desired
  achievement party.
\item
  Re-cross the palace, summon the wagon again, and climb the mountain.
\item
  Defeat all seven forms of Necrosaro without leaving battle.
\item
  Return to Zenithia with the Master Dragon and leave the castle to
  complete the ending.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Necrosaro's Palace}\label{necrosaros-palace}

\textbf{Recommended Hero level:} 40 or higher\\
\textbf{Fixed treasure:} Small Medal, Sage's Stone, Lifeforce Nuts,
Staff of Thunder\\
\textbf{Required key item at the rear exit:} Baron's Horn\\
\textbf{Primary hazard:} Damaging floors and roaming Clay Dolls

Enter the central palace from the Dark World after all four sections of
its barrier have vanished. The wagon cannot follow the active party
through the palace interior, so choose four durable members for the
crossing. \textbf{Hero / Alena / Ragnar / Cristo} remains the safest
formation.

Three Clay Dolls patrol the entrance room. Contact starts an inescapable
battle, and their Defeat spell can cause an unnecessary loss before the
final boss. Cast StepGuard, move east from the entrance, and follow the
eastern wall north to the corridor in the northeast corner. Continue
through the next room, walk south to its far end, and climb the stairs.

\subsection{Palace Enemy Reference}\label{palace-enemy-reference}

\begin{guidetable}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
