\section{Small Medal 32}\label{small-medal-32}

After climbing from the entrance route, walk outside, continue
northeast, and re-enter the palace. Follow the hallway to the room
containing a spirit and two staircases.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Take the descending stairs into the poisoned basement.
\item
  Cast StepGuard before leaving the safe tiles.
\item
  Walk all the way south across the damaging floor.
\item
  Open the chest for \textbf{Small Medal 32}.
\item
  Return to the room with the spirit and two staircases.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{guidenote}

\textbf{Achievement Check: Small Medal Collector}

\textbf{Running total: 32/32.} If every routed medal has been collected,
\texttt{Small\ Medal\ Collector} should unlock here. If the total is
lower, do not continue to the ending until the audit in Side Activities
and Final Checks identifies the missing medal.

\end{guidenote}

This is the final physical Small Medal in the North American NES release
and completes the 32-medal achievement route.

\section{Hidden Passage and the Sage's
Stone}\label{hidden-passage-and-the-sages-stone}

From the spirit room, take the eastern staircase upward and follow the
linear path until reaching the first elevator. Ignore the elevator for
now and climb the staircase immediately north of it.

On the next floor, walk directly north between the pair of statues. The
wall conceals a passable corridor that is not visible from the room.
Continue east through the hidden room, descend the stairs, follow the
passage to another staircase, and then walk south through the exterior
route. Open the chest at the end for the \textbf{Sage's Stone}.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Hidden Treasure - Necrosaro's Palace}

\textbf{Trigger:} Pass between the statues, follow the concealed route,
and open the chest containing the Sage's Stone.

\end{achievementalert}

The Sage's Stone casts Healus when used in battle and consumes no MP. It
is the most important item for the final battle. Give it to the Hero for
the standard and two-member Hero routes so its group healing can be
commanded every round when needed.

\section{Remaining Palace Treasure}\label{remaining-palace-treasure}

Return to the room where the hidden passage begins. Take the
southeastern exit and follow the route to a room containing two chests.
Open them for \textbf{Lifeforce Nuts} and the \textbf{Staff of Thunder}.

The Staff of Thunder has 29 attack power and can be equipped by Brey or
Mara. Its combat-use effect is more valuable than its physical attack,
but direct spell damage becomes less dependable during Necrosaro's later
forms. Store it if inventory space is tight.

Return to the first elevator that was skipped after the spirit room.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Ride the first elevator down one floor.
\item
  Walk south, then follow the eastern path into the next room.
\item
  Walk north, climb the stairs, and follow the path to the second
  elevator.
\item
  Ride the second elevator down to the previous floor.
\item
  Return up the nearby stairs. The lowered elevator now forms a walkable
  platform over the former obstruction.
\item
  Cross the top of the elevator and climb the staircase in the northeast
  corner.
\item
  Climb the next staircase and walk west into the room with water in its
  center.
\item
  Travel southwest to the exit, then continue west through the final
  doorway.
\end{enumerate}

The party emerges on the Dark World map at the base of Necrosaro's
mountain. Use the \textbf{Baron's Horn} here to call the wagon and
reunite the full roster. The horn must be used outside the palace; it
cannot solve the separation while the party is still inside.

\section{Creating a Repeatable Final-Boss
Save}\label{creating-a-repeatable-final-boss-save}

Do not climb the mountain immediately after the first treasure sweep.
Cast Return to the Last Refuge, restore everyone, and make an in-game
save. The palace treasure flags remain set, so future crossings can
ignore every side branch and follow only the elevator route to the rear
exit.

Each Necrosaro victory proceeds directly into the ending. After earning
one set of achievements, reload this Last Refuge save, cross the cleared
palace again, use the Baron's Horn at the rear exit, and configure the
next party. This save also preserves a point before the ending for the
cleanup activities in the following section.

\section{Final Equipment and
Inventory}\label{final-equipment-and-inventory}

The standard party remains \textbf{Hero / Alena / Ragnar / Cristo}.
Exact equipment depends on purchases and medal rewards, but prioritize
durability and reliable physical damage.

Keep the \textbf{Dress of Radiance} off anyone who must receive healing
because its reflection can reject friendly spells. Carry a \textbf{Leaf
of the World Tree} for guaranteed revival and a \textbf{Dew of the World
Tree} as an emergency full-party resource. The Sandglass of Regression
is optional insurance if a disastrous sequence needs to be replayed.

After crossing the palace, use the Baron's Horn before arranging the
active party. Confirm every character not intended for the attempt is in
the wagon, including Doran when making a strict two-member attempt.

\section{Achievement Party Plans}\label{achievement-party-plans}

Keep the same chosen formation from the opening round through
Necrosaro's defeat. Do not rely on moving disallowed characters out
immediately before the final blow.

The combined attempt is the shortest route if both challenge triggers
evaluate the final formation as described. Preserve the pre-boss save
regardless: if only one challenge unlocks, use the dedicated formation
for the remaining achievement. Levels above the normal recommendation,
strong medal equipment, and learned battle behavior make either
restricted clear much more reliable.

For the no-Hero attempt, Normal tactics provide the best balance while
the party is healthy; switch to Defensive when group damage accumulates.
Cristo and Nara offer redundant healing and revival while Alena and
Ragnar supply offense. For the two-member Hero attempt, the Hero should
use the Sage's Stone frequently and reserve MP for Healall, Healusall,
and revival. Alena ends forms faster through critical hits, while Ragnar
is the more durable partner.

\section{Necrosaro's Mountain}\label{necrosaros-mountain}

With the wagon present and the desired active formation selected, walk
north into the mountain. The ascent is a short winding path with
occasional Flamadog encounters. Preserve HP and MP rather than grinding
here. At the summit, approach Necrosaro to begin one continuous
seven-form battle.

There is no recovery break, inventory access, or save opportunity
between forms. Each transformation receives a new HP pool, for a total
of \textbf{5,696 HP} across the battle.

\section{Boss: Necrosaro}\label{boss-necrosaro}

\end{guidetable}

Necrosaro awards no EXP or gold. The achievement triggers are tied to
defeating the final form, not to an end-of-battle reward screen.

\subsection{Forms 1 and 2: The Arms}\label{forms-1-and-2-the-arms}

Begin on Normal tactics. Form 1 attacks twice, so use the Sage's Stone
whenever more than one character needs meaningful healing. Alena and
Ragnar should attack continuously while Cristo supports them. If using
Bikill, apply it to the strongest physical attacker, but do not
sacrifice urgent recovery for setup.

After the first arm is destroyed, Form 2 acts only once per round. This
is the safest part of the battle. Restore the party, revive anyone who
fell, and stabilize MP before removing the second arm. Avoid rushing the
transformation while a healer is weak.

\subsection{Form 3: Head and Magic}\label{form-3-head-and-magic}

Without both arms, Necrosaro turns to Firebane, Snowstorm, Increase, and
sweet breath. If the Hero is active, use the Zenithian Sword as an item
to remove Increase when the defense boost materially slows the
attackers. Cast Awake when sleep disables an essential healer or damage
dealer.

Barrier helps against spell damage if the AI supplies it, but remember
that later forms erase support effects. Keep direct damage moving and
preserve enough healing for the more dangerous transformations.

\subsection{Form 4: The First
Transformation}\label{form-4-the-first-transformation}

After losing his original head, Necrosaro becomes a green creature with
a new face. He can erase active spell effects with a freezing wave,
recover HP through Meditation, and damage the party with scorching gas
or violent blaze.

Do not enter a long cycle of rebuilding every removed buff. Heal first,
restore only high-value effects when the party has breathing room, and
pressure the 700 HP pool so Meditation cannot prolong the form
indefinitely.

\subsection{Forms 5 and 6: Arms and Legs
Restored}\label{forms-5-and-6-arms-and-legs-restored}

Form 5 regrows its arms and returns to two actions per round. Scorching
gas can damage the entire active party before a healer responds. Use the
Sage's Stone whenever group HP is uneven and reserve Healusall for
rounds where free healing will not be enough.

Form 6 adds the legs but follows the same basic pattern. Near the end of
its 700 HP pool, slow down long enough to restore the party. The next
form opens with Bounce and has the battle's highest sustained
group-damage potential.

\subsection{Form 7: Final Form}\label{form-7-final-form}

The final form begins with Bounce, acts twice, uses violent blaze and
icy breath, and can erase support effects with the freezing wave. Do not
let Mara, Brey, or another caster repeatedly throw reflected attack
spells. Physical attackers should carry the offense while the Hero uses
the Sage's Stone on most damaged rounds.

Treat a freezing wave that removes few or no active buffs as a
low-damage opportunity. Attack aggressively on that turn, but keep
enough HP to survive two consecutive breath attacks. If Cristo is
present, Defensive tactics can improve recovery consistency when the
party falls behind.

Defeating the 1,023 HP final form ends the battle and begins Necrosaro's
collapse.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Is This The End?}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Necrosaro's seventh and final form.

\end{achievementalert}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: We Don't Need Another Hero}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Necrosaro while the Hero remains outside the
active party. Keep the Hero in the wagon for the complete battle to
satisfy the condition cleanly.

\end{achievementalert}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Just the Two of Us}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat Necrosaro with exactly two active party
members. Keep all other characters and Doran out of the active formation
for the complete battle.

\end{achievementalert}

The normal achievement unlocks on every successful clear. The two
challenge alerts apply only when their stated formation was maintained.
Reload the Last Refuge save after the ending to make another attempt;
unlocked RetroAchievements remain credited even though the game itself
returns to the pre-boss save.

\section{The Ending}\label{the-ending}

Necrosaro's mountain begins to collapse after the battle. The Master
Dragon rescues the party and returns everyone to Zenithia, where fallen
allies are restored and the assembled heroes are thanked for ending the
threat.

Explore Zenithia for the residents' final dialogue if desired, then
leave the castle to commit to the ending sequence. The balloon carries
the companions home in turn:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Alena, Cristo, and Brey return to the restored Santeem Castle.
\item
  Ragnar returns to Burland.
\item
  Taloon reunites with his family in Endor.
\item
  Nara and Mara visit Kievs and then return to Monbaraba.
\item
  The Hero returns alone to the ruins of Hometown.
\end{enumerate}

At Hometown, Celia reappears. Ragnar, Alena, Cristo, Brey, Taloon, Nara,
and Mara then arrive to reunite with the Hero before the credits.

There is no playable postgame after the credits. Use the preserved Last
Refuge save for any remaining achievement cleanup or additional
Necrosaro formations.

\section{Final Battle Completion
Check}\label{final-battle-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Palace achievement:} Hidden Treasure - Necrosaro's Palace
  unlocked from the Sage's Stone route.
\item
  \textbf{Story achievement:} Is This The End? unlocked after defeating
  the seventh form.
\item
  \textbf{Challenge achievements:} We Don't Need Another Hero and Just
  the Two of Us unlocked through dedicated or combined formation
  attempts.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} 32/32 routed medals found; Small Medal
  Collector unlocked if every earlier medal was collected.
\item
  \textbf{Critical items:} Sage's Stone secured and Baron's Horn used
  outside the palace.
\item
  \textbf{Final boss:} All seven forms defeated, totaling 5,696 HP.
\item
  \textbf{Ending:} Zenithia dialogue completed and the castle exited to
  begin the homecoming sequence.
\item
  \textbf{Next destination:} Reload the pre-boss save for Side
  Activities and Final Checks if any optional achievement remains.
\end{itemize}

definitive final-boss clear. Players who already earned one or more of
these achievements can use the corresponding subsection as confirmation
and move on.

The efficient cleanup order is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Check whether \texttt{Small\ Medal\ Collector} unlocked when Small
  Medal 32 was opened in Necrosaro's Palace.
\item

\item
  Build and hold 10,000 Casino coins at Endor.
\item
  Hunt a King Metal near Gottside or in the Final Cave.
\item
  Return to the Last Refuge, restore the party, and make a fresh in-game
  save.
\item
  Complete any outstanding Necrosaro party-composition victory, then
  finish the ending.
\end{enumerate}

\section{High Roller: Endor Casino}\label{high-roller-endor-casino}

\textbf{Location:} Basement beneath the Endor inn\\
\textbf{Chapter 5 exchange rate:} 20G per Casino coin\\
\textbf{Required balance:} 10,000 coins at one time\\
\textbf{Recommended game:} 100-coin poker

Casino coins are shared across chapters, so any balance retained from
Alena or Taloon reduces the Chapter 5 grind. Speak with the exchange
attendant to buy a starting reserve. Purchasing all 10,000 coins
directly costs \textbf{200,000G}, making direct conversion the fastest
option if the party has accumulated enough unused gold.

The casino offers slot machines, poker, and the Monster Arena. Slots and
the arena provide less control over an individual attempt. Poker
supports 1-, 10-, and 100-coin antes and gives access to a
double-or-nothing sequence after a winning hand, making it the most
practical route to a five-digit balance.

Mirror of Ra & 2,500 & Reveals transformed enemies but is not required
for the final route. \\
Meteorite Armband & 4,000 & Excellent speed accessory for a healer or
physical attacker. \\
Metal Babble Shield & 50,000 & Premium caster shield and the best
long-term casino target. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\end{guidetable}

Do not redeem any prize until the displayed balance reaches at least
\textbf{10,000} and the achievement notification appears. Spending below
the threshold before the check can delay the unlock even if 10,000 total
coins have been earned over time.

\subsection{Poker and Double-or-Nothing
Route}\label{poker-and-double-or-nothing-route}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Record the adventure normally in Endor before beginning the session.
\item
  Buy enough coins to absorb several maximum-ante hands without
  immediately reaching zero.
\item
  Use the 100-coin poker table once the reserve can support normal
  losing streaks.
\item
  After a winning hand, choose double-or-nothing and predict whether the
  next card will be higher or lower.
\item
  Each correct prediction doubles the current award. Collect after a
  meaningful gain rather than risking the entire chain indefinitely.
\item
  Leave the casino and make another normal in-game save after reaching a
  new balance milestone.
\item
  Reload the normal save after a major losing session, then resume from
  the banked balance.
\end{enumerate}

This route uses only Dragon Warrior IV's normal save and reset cycle. In
Hardcore mode, do not load emulator save states or use rewind, slow
motion, frame advance, or cheats. Fast-forward is explicitly allowed by
current RetroAchievements rules.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: High Roller}

\textbf{Trigger:} Hold at least 10,000 Casino coins simultaneously. Wait
for the unlock before purchasing the Meteorite Armband or another prize.

\end{achievementalert}

After the achievement unlocks, spend only what the remaining final-boss
plans can spare. A second Meteorite Armband is immediately useful; the
Metal Babble Shield requires a much longer grind and is optional.

\section{The One That Didn't Get Away: King Metal
Hunt}\label{the-one-that-didnt-get-away-king-metal-hunt}

\textbf{Preferred location:} Hills northeast of Gottside\\
\textbf{Alternative location:} Final Cave\\
\textbf{Recovery base:} Gottside or the Last Refuge\\
\textbf{Required result:} Defeat one King Metal before it flees

\end{guidetable}

The Gottside hills are the cleaner hunting ground because Return
provides quick access to town and the surrounding encounters are less
costly than those in the Final Cave. The Final Cave is a reasonable
alternative while leveling for the restricted Necrosaro attempts, but
its ordinary enemies make failed hunts more expensive.

Build the active party around attacks that can bypass or work through
metal-monster defense:

\begin{guidetable}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 2\tabcolsep) * \real{0.3777}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 2\tabcolsep) * \real{0.6223}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
\begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
\color{GuideGreen}\bfseries Character or Item
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
\color{GuideGreen}\bfseries Role Against King Metal
\end{minipage} \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
Alena with Stilleto Earrings & Two attacks plus her naturally high
critical-hit rate provide the best immediate victory chance. \\
Hero or Ragnar with Metal Babble Sword & Supplies reliable metal-target
damage when ordinary weapons would deal little or none. \\
Brey with Poison Needle & Fixed chip damage remains useful, and its
instant-kill effect can end the encounter. \\
Mara with Poison Needle & Can contribute fixed damage or, under
Offensive tactics, attempt BeDragon. \\
BeDragon & Costs a transformation turn; the following breath can kill a
surviving King Metal if it has not fled. \\
Sandglass of Regression & Rewinds an ongoing battle once per day; the
supplied NES sources describe either a return to the beginning or
several rounds earlier. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\end{guidetable}

Use \textbf{Hero / Alena / Brey / Mara} when all listed tools are
available. Put the Metal Babble Sword on the Hero, Stilleto Earrings on
Alena, and Poison Needles on Brey and Mara. Offensive tactics make Mara
more likely to choose BeDragon, but the immediate attacks from Alena,
the Hero, and Brey remain important because the target may flee before
Mara's transformed turn.

Focus every action on one King Metal. Do not divide damage across
multiple targets unless a group spell or breath will finish all
survivors. A critical hit from Alena is the cleanest result; otherwise,
accumulate the seven required points of damage before the enemy escapes.

The Sandglass of Regression is a normal in-game item rather than
emulator rewind. Use it while the encounter is still active; once every
target has fled and the battle has closed, there is no battle left to
rewind.

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: The One That Didn't Get Away}

\textbf{Trigger:} Defeat a King Metal. Metal Slimes and Metal Babbles do
not satisfy this achievement.

\end{achievementalert}

The 30,010 EXP reward is useful preparation for the two-member Necrosaro
clear, but one confirmed victory is sufficient. Stop the hunt once the
achievement unlocks unless additional levels are specifically needed.

\section{Small Medal Collector Audit}\label{small-medal-collector-audit}

The North American NES release contains \textbf{32 Small Medals}.
Spending medals at the Medal King's castle does not undo their
discovery, so audit locations rather than comparing the current
inventory count. If the route was followed exactly, Small Medal 32 in
Necrosaro's Palace already triggered the achievement.

One of the two Konenber ship medals becomes permanently unavailable
after the Great Lighthouse sequence. If either was skipped before the
lighthouse, an earlier save or another playthrough may be required. The
remaining routed locations can be revisited from the preserved pre-boss
save.

\endlastfoot

room, and search the dresser. \\

shipyard, go downstairs, and search the dresser before completing the

3 & Mintos & Search the ground immediately west of the central well. \\
4 & Shrine east of Mintos & Follow the grassy strip between the outer

pot along the north wall. \\
6 & Small island shack north of Haville & Search the right pot; later
references to this shack describe this same medal and must not be

7 & Endor Castle & At night, unlock the royal bedroom and search the
far-right dresser. \\
8 & Seaside Village & Search the ground directly south of the central
statue. \\
9 & Stancia & Enter the northeast house, climb to the second floor, and
search the pots. \\
10 & Stancia Castle & During daytime, search the dresser in the
far-right room on eastern 1F. \\
11 & Gardenbur Castle & Search the pots in the western first-floor
kitchen. \\
12 & Cave Southeast of Gardenbur & Follow the narrow eastern path to its
end and open the chest. \\
13 & Branca Castle & Unlock the northeast jail treasury with the Final
Key and open its chests. \\
14 & Haville jail & Search the pot in the first cell. \\
15 & Haville jail & Search the pot in the third cell occupied by the
Yeti. \\
16 & Shrine of Breaking Waves & After the fourth row of rock pillars,
land on the left bank and follow the concealed western route. \\
17 & Shrine of Breaking Waves & Open the two northwest chests
downstairs; one holds the medal and the other the Zenithian Armor. \\
18 & Cascade Cave & At the first lower-floor split, follow the northern
branch to the chest. \\
19 & Royal Crypt & Take the eastern side corridor north without touching
the right-side arrows, then enter the western room. \\
20 & Riverton & On the central island, stand two tiles south of the
resident and search the southernmost grass tile. \\
21 & Shrine of the Colossus & Inside the lower body, follow the outer
route behind the central stairs to the chest. \\
22 & Dire Palace & Search the pots in the basement kitchen. \\
23 & Dire Palace & In the concealed treasure room, open the northern
chests for the medal and Aeolus' Shield. \\
24 & Aktemto Mine B4 & At the eastern dead end, search the southern edge
against the water. \\
25 & Esturk's Palace & Open the chest in the first small room north of
the entrance. \\
26 & Esturk's Palace & Follow the lower route to the two-chest room; one
is a Mimic and the other holds the medal. \\
27 & Gottside & Descend into the northeast underground room and search
the eastern dresser. \\
28 & Zenithian Tower & Take the southwest stair branches to the dead-end
treasure room. \\
29 & Zenithian Castle & Enter the room right of the renewable Dew room
and search the dressers. \\
30 & Final Cave & Use the entrance-floor arrow tiles to reach the
enclosed southern chest. \\
31 & Gigademon's shrine & Follow the exterior tombstone path clockwise
and search the far northwest corner. \\
32 & Necrosaro's Palace & Descend from the spirit room, cast StepGuard,
cross the poisoned floor, and open the southern chest. \\

}

\end{guidetable}

\begin{achievementalert}

\textbf{Achievement Alert: Small Medal Collector}

\textbf{Trigger:} Collect the last missing medal among the 32 routed
locations. On the main route, this occurs when the Necrosaro's Palace
chest raises the running total from 31/32 to 32/32.

\end{achievementalert}

If the achievement remains locked, compare the table against the route
in numerical order. Start with medals 1 and 2 in Konenber, then check
searched pots, dressers, and ground tiles; chest medals are easier to
recognize as already opened. Do not count a later reference to an
already collected location as another medal.

\section{Final Pre-Necrosaro Check}\label{final-pre-necrosaro-check}

Return to the Last Refuge after cleanup, restore HP and MP, and record
the adventure. Before crossing Necrosaro's Palace for the final time,
verify:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{High Roller:} The achievement unlocked while the Casino coin
  balance displayed at least 10,000.
\item
  \textbf{King Metal:} The One That Didn't Get Away unlocked from an
  actual King Metal victory.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} Small Medal Collector unlocked and the
  32-location audit is complete.
\item
  \textbf{Palace treasure:} Sage's Stone, Staff of Thunder, Lifeforce
  Nuts, and Small Medal 32 were collected.
\item
  \textbf{Recovery items:} A Leaf of the World Tree and Dew of the World
  Tree are available where possible.
\item
  \textbf{Key item:} The Baron's Horn is carried through the palace and
  used at the rear exit.
\item
  \textbf{Equipment:} The final party and reserve members have their
  intended weapons, armor, shields, helmets, and accessories.
\item
  \textbf{Challenge formation:} The active party matches the next
  Necrosaro achievement attempt before entering battle.
\item
  \textbf{Save:} A normal in-game save exists at the Last Refuge for
  further attempts.
\end{itemize}

The final battle and ending route remain in the preceding section. This
cleanup section ends at the pre-boss checkpoint and does not repeat
Necrosaro's seven forms.

\section{Side Activity Completion
Check}\label{side-activity-completion-check}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Achievements:} High Roller, The One That Didn't Get Away, and
  Small Medal Collector unlocked.
\item
  \textbf{Achievement points:} 60 points available from this section's
  three objectives.
\item
  \textbf{Casino:} A 10,000-coin balance was reached before prizes were
  redeemed.
\item
  \textbf{King Metal:} At least one defeated; further hunting is
  optional leveling.
\item
  \textbf{Small Medals:} All 32 routed locations confirmed, including
  both Konenber ship medals collected before their cutoff.
\item
  \textbf{Final save:} Last Refuge save updated after cleanup and before
  the final battle.
\item
  \textbf{Next section:} Reference Appendices.
\end{itemize}

\begin{landscape}

\chapter{Reference Appendices}\label{reference-appendices}

These appendices condense the route's recurring information into lookup
tables. They do not introduce new progression steps. When a reference
entry and a walkthrough instruction serve different purposes, follow the
detailed walkthrough section for navigation and use the appendix for
quick verification.

\section{Permanent Character
Reference}\label{permanent-character-reference}

Chapter 5 permits direct commands only for the Hero. Every other active
character follows the selected tactic and develops resistance knowledge
through repeated encounters. Party advice therefore considers both a
character's abilities and how reliably the AI uses them.

Try Out & Situational experiments with unusual actions or items &
Deliberately unpredictable and unsafe for serious bosses. \\
\end{longtable}
}

\end{guidetable}

limited or expensive item. Give the best unique equipment to the active
challenge party first, then equip reserves from the listed alternatives.

\section{Notable Monster Treasures}\label{notable-monster-treasures}

The supplied Monster Treasures guide records which item each random
monster can leave behind or Taloon can steal. It does not provide drop
probabilities, so no odds are claimed here. This table favors permanent
stat items, uncommon equipment, and late-game rewards over ordinary
herbs and basic shop gear.
